# One full equality-chart search: derivation and frozen plan

Status: joint UNVERIFIED research derivation and numerical-discovery plan.
Owner max3, continuation round after completed44, ceiling45/attempted46.
Authority: current TASK.md and master guidance59c43473b63b1939. Exactly
ONE mathematical job,30 conservatively charged seconds; no retry, formal
gate, installation, consult, native agent, or automatic renewal.

The original exact-profile Goal, W=2R, and the sufficient threshold in
accepted4c92d3fc4eb6153e are unchanged. The numerical target is an actual
length5 word with A>Gamma5=7977607/806000, preferably A>=99/10. A numerical
endpoint is only a candidate. No seed or original-target theorem is asserted.

## 1. Correctness inputs and reproduced equality chart

The accepted inputs are04219554e41d9750, Section10, and1252b8823bb5a290,
proof paragraph1. They give the original finite interpolation orientation
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0 and the certificate identity

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_5)>,
 V_h=2sum_(i<5)h_i(f_i-f_5)+sum_(i<5)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_5||^2,
 s=sum h_i, A=1+s,
 T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
 L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_4,2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<5,-s(s+2)), M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2,

provided L^T e=omega. All six gradients are independent formal variables,
e is the six-vector of ones, and Lambda has zero diagonal. All30 original
ordered off-diagonal coefficients must be nonnegative, and M must be PSD
on the whole R^6. The coefficient identity is also checked by separating
position terms sym(L(T-I)) and squared-gradient terms sym(L)-diag(omega)/2.
Adding the diagonal part of V_h yields exactly D+sym(LT).

Put z=Te, Z=diag(z), d(X)=Xe-X^Te, and d_z(X)=Xz-ZX^Te=d(XZ).
Both divergences have total sum zero. Direct coordinate calculation gives
2De+T^T omega=-Z omega: if p_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, its i<5 coordinate is
2h_i(2-h_i)-2h_i+2h_i(p_i+h_i)=2h_i(1+p_i)=-z_i omega_i.
The last coordinate is -2s(s+2)+2s=-2s(s+1).
Thus, when d(Lambda)=omega,

 2Me=Lz-Zomega=-d_z(Lambda).

Consequently the two original equalities are equivalent to
d(Lambda)=omega and d_z(Lambda)=0. Moreover, under the first equality,
e^TMe=0 because e^TDe=-(s^2+sum h_i^2) and z^Tomega=s^2+sum h_i^2.
If M is PSD, the quadratic (e+tv)^TM(e+tv) has zero constant term;
nonnegativity for both signs of t forces e^TMv=0 for all v. Hence Me=0
is necessary for every original feasible certificate and costs no solutions.

Use the path v=(0,1,2,5,4,3), oriented successively. For edge u->w let
S be the initial path segment through u, Delta=z_u-z_w, and N have all20
independent off-tree ordered entries, zero diagonal and tree entries.
Define

 alpha=sum_(i in S)omega_i-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij-N_ji),
 beta=-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij z_j-N_ji z_i).
 Lambda_uw=(z_u alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_wu=(z_w alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_ij=N_ij off the tree.

This solves a-b=alpha and z_w a-z_u b=beta. Every tree cut of each
residual divergence is zero. Rooting the tree, a leaf residual is zero
by its singleton subtree cut; subtracting zero child subtree sums proves
each parent residual zero, and the root follows from total sum zero.
Conversely every original equality array must satisfy these two cut
equations, whose determinant is -Delta, so its dependent entries are
uniquely the displayed formulas. The inverse just reads the20 off-tree
entries. They and all five h_i remain independent variables.

The successive cuts are {0},{0,1},{0,1,2},{0,1,2,5},{0,1,2,5,4}; their
Delta values are -h_0,-h_1,-(h_2+h_3+h_4),h_4,h_3. They are nonzero in
the registered box. This is a coordinate basis, not a support assumption.
At a zero Delta the actual compatibility condition is beta=z_u alpha;
if compatible the whole pair is (t+alpha,t), otherwise no completion
exists at those off-tree coordinates. These singular cases lie outside
this search box and are not declared infeasible for the original problem.
In particular a box restriction is not a global theorem about the cone.

## 2. Full-space factor equivalence, including singular factors

Write B for the leading5-by-5 block of M and J=[I_5;-1_5^T]. From Me=0
one obtains all final row and column entries, giving M=JBJ^T. For arbitrary
independent g_0,...,g_5 its quadratic form is
(g_(0:4)-g_5 1)^TB(g_(0:4)-g_5 1). It follows in both directions that
M>=0 iff B>=0. This identity does not identify any two gradients.

A symmetric PSD B has a lower triangular factor C with nonnegative
diagonal, B=CC^T, including the singular case. Induct on the first pivot
of the current PSD residual. A positive pivot p allows a square completion
and leaves the PSD Schur residual. If p=0, positivity of each two-variable
quadratic forces its whole off-diagonal row zero; set that factor column
zero and continue on the remaining principal submatrix. A negative pivot,
or zero pivot with nonzero off-diagonal row, is incompatible with PSD.
Conversely any CC^T is PSD. This establishes exact equivalence without
fixing rank. The full factor is F=JC; its last row is minus the sum of
the first five rows. All15 lower factor entries vary in the search.

## 3. Reproducing the specified strict start, without adopting a draft fact

Let a=sqrt2, rho=a-1, lambda=1+a,
b=(-a+sqrt(10+8a))/2, p=a+b, t=99/100, delta=1/100,
X=ta, Y=tb, S=X+Y, C0=1+S, d=rho C0-delta,
h_*=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y), A_*=2C0+d,
c=(C0+d)/(C0-d), k=1+2c,
tau=delta(2a C0-delta)/(C0-d), eta=2tau,
u=2S(1+X)/Y, v0=2SC0/Y, epsilon=1/10000000.
Here C0 is a scalar capacity, C above is a matrix factor, and v0 is a
scalar coefficient, distinct from the path v.
The identity b(a+b)=2(1+a) follows by squaring the defining root.

The two-step child (X,Y) has multipliers Lambda_01=2,
Lambda_10=2(1+X), Lambda_12=u, Lambda_21=v0, other entries zero.
Its flow is (-2X,-2Y,2S), since v0-u=2S. Direct substitution gives the
path-Laplacian remainder with edge weights

 w0=2-X^2=2delta(1+t),
 w1=(S/Y)[2-Y^2+(2-Y)X]=2(p/b)delta(1+lambda t).

For two such children and the bridge1+d, the complete baseline array is

 [0,       2,       0,       0,          0,         0]
 [2(1+X),  0,       u,       0,          0,         0]
 [0,       v0,      0,       0,          0,         2cd+eta]
 [0,       0,       2cX,     0,          2k,        2cX]
 [0,       0,       2cY,     2k(1+X),    0,         ku+2cY]
 [0,       0,       2c+eta,  0,          kv0,       0].

Its last four divergences are respectively
v0-u+2c(d-C0)=-2(1+d), 4cX-2kX=-2X,
4cY+2kX+k(u-v0)=-2Y, k(v0-u)+2c(2-C0-d)=2s.
The first two are the child divergences. Thus all six flows are checked.

Here is a full quadratic check of its remainder. Write
U_L=g0-g1, V_L=g1-g2, U_R=g3-g4, V_R=g4-g5,
zeta=g2-g5, Kvec=X U_R+S V_R.
Expanding the original matrix, using c(C0-d)=C0+d and
d(2C0+d)-C0^2=-delta(2a C0-delta), gives

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
       +k(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
       +tau[(C0-d)||zeta||^2-2<zeta,Kvec>].

One way to check every mixed term is to use
E=XQ_32+YQ_42+Q_52+dQ_25+XQ_35+YQ_45.
The tail-to-tail terms cancel between its two tail sums; bridge-to-tail
terms cancel against dQ_25; tail-to-terminal terms against Q_52; the
bridge-to-terminal terms in Q_52 and dQ_25 cancel. Its remaining gradient
part is X(1-X)||g3||^2+Y(1-Y)||g4||^2+
[(C0-d)/2-d^2]||g2||^2+(C0+d)||g5||^2/2.
The identity V_h=V_left+kV_right-2cE+
c[d(2C0+d)-C0^2](||g5||^2-||g2||^2) follows by matching its values and
diagonals. Adding eta(Q_25+Q_52), with
Q_25+Q_52=-d||zeta||^2-(C0+d)<zeta,g5>-<zeta,Kvec>, gives exactly the
displayed M0[g]. Thus the full matrix, rather than only its diagonal,
has been reproduced.

Put R=X^2/w0+S^2/w1=Rbar/delta,
Rbar=t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)], q=tau/(C0-d), sigma=k-qR,
E0=zeta-Kvec/(C0-d). Square completion gives the exact identity

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
 +sigma(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
 +q||S sqrt(w0/w1)U_R-X sqrt(w1/w0)V_R||^2
 +tau(C0-d)||E0||^2.

The cross term in the fourth square is -2qXS<U_R,V_R>, and its other
terms equal q[R(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)-||Kvec||^2], verifying the
identity. For quantitative bounds, the increasing polynomial
F(x)=x^2+ax-2(1+a) changes sign between8/5 and161/100, while
7/5<a<71/50. Hence3<p<303/100,397/100<C0<403/100,
1<X<71/50,1<Y<161/100,3/2<d<17/10,39/100<d/C0<21/50,
(29/50)C0<C0-d<(61/100)C0<3.
Both terms in Rbar increase for t>=0, so Rbar<=lambda/2.
Consequently k>339/61>11/2 and
qR<(7/2)C0/[(29/50)^2 C0^2]=8750/(841C0)<3, giving sigma>5/2.
Also w0,w1>3delta, sigma w0,sigma w1>(15/2)delta, and
tau(C0-d)>11delta. The bounds X/(C0-d)<2/3 and S/(C0-d)<4/3 imply
||zeta||^2<=3||E0||^2+(4/3)||U_R||^2+(16/3)||V_R||^2.
Therefore M0[g]>=delta E_tree, where E_tree is the sum of the five
squared differences along(0,1,2,5,4,3).
For sum g_i=0, ||g||^2=(1/6)sum_(i<j)(g_i-g_j)^2. Bounding each pair
along that path gives edge coefficients15,24,27,24,15, hence
||g||^2<=(9/2)E_tree and M0>=(2delta/9)P on e-perp,
where P=I-ee^T/6. This proves the baseline margin directly.

To fill every missing multiplier, let K0=6I-ee^T and
F_l=sum_(i in S_l)(K0z)_i, w_l=F_l/(z_u-z_w), gamma_l=1-w_l.
Set H=K0-sum_l w_l(e_u-e_w)(e_u-e_w)^T. Then He=0.
Also Hz=0: at vertex v_l the edge sum has coordinate F_l-F_(l-1),
which is (K0z)_(v_l), with F_-1=F_5=0. Add epsilon on both directions
of every non-tree pair and epsilon gamma_l on both directions of each
tree pair. This changes the Laplacian by epsilon H, preserving both
flow and Me=0. The full changed remainder is M=M0+epsilon sym(HT).

For an explicit construction let Zsum=2+C0+3A_*-2Y. The five w_l are
(Zsum-6)/X, [2Zsum-6(2+X)]/Y,
[3Zsum-6(2+X+C0)]/(C0+d),
[6(2+X+C0+A_*)-4Zsum]/Y,
[Zsum-6(C0+1+d)]/X.
These specify every entry of the dense array by the preceding additions.
Each cut has at most9 crossing pairs, each potential difference has
absolute value<s<9, and each tree difference has magnitude>1. Thus
|w_l|<81, |gamma_l|<82. Every original tree coefficient is at least2;
after filling, all30 coefficients are >=epsilon. The direct bounds
c<5/2,k<6,tau<5delta,u<10,v0<16 give all final coefficients<97.
Moreover ||H||<816 and ||T||<=||T||_F<sqrt141<12, so
||sym(HT)||<10000. Since epsilon=delta/100000,
M>(2delta/9-delta/10)P>(delta/9)P=(1/900)P on e-perp.
The upper bound ||M0||<342delta follows from four edge forms bounded
by252delta and the bridge form bounded by90delta; adding the perturbation
gives M<(7/2)P. For g=(y,0), ||Pg||^2>=||y||^2/6, so B>=I/5400.
No numerical evaluation has been used in this derivation.

The chart's starting coordinates are only
N_32=N_35=2cX+epsilon, N_42=2cY+epsilon, all other17 equal epsilon.
The baseline cut alpha values are -2X,-2S,2c(d-1),2kS-2cY,2kX;
the beta values are0,0,2cC0S,2c(C0S+A_*X),2cX(C0+A_*).
Filling changes beta by -epsilon Delta_l gamma_l and leaves alpha
unchanged. Substitution into the cut formulas reproduces all ten tree
entries of the explicit dense array. This proves exact chart readback.
The initial numerical job must additionally compare these independently
constructed arrays and check the actual original matrix and margins.

Its capacity A_*=a+(99/100)[(1+a)(a+b)+1] is below48763/5000<49/5<Gamma5.
It is the required strict start, not a usable improving seed.

## 4. Frozen numerical specification

Implementation: run.wl, executed ONCE by the existing Wolfram evaluator.
All machine mathematics, including model construction, initial arrays,
factorization, optimization, original readbacks, and encoding, occurs in
that single call. No code is evaluated for calibration beforehand.

Variables: five independent h; all20 off-tree ordered coefficients in
lexicographic order; all15 entries of a lower triangular5-by-5 factor C.
The ten dependent Lambda entries use the exact cuts above. No reflection,
support, repeated row, zero remainder, fixed rank, or one-parameter path
is imposed. The relation h=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y) holds ONLY at the initial point.
Equality constraints are the15 lower triangular entries of B-CC^T=0.
All30 multiplier inequalities remain imposed, including tree entries.

Search box:1/4<=each h_i<=6;0<=each original Lambda_ij<=400 (i!=j);
-100<=each lower factor entry<=100, diagonal>=0; additionally every entry
of the FULL6-by-5 factor JC is in[-100,100]. These are search restrictions,
not proved global bounds. The registered objective is maximize1+sum h_i.

Method: one FindMinimum call on its negative, Method->InteriorPoint,
WorkingPrecision->MachinePrecision, AccuracyGoal->8, PrecisionGoal->8,
MaxIterations->600. Initial point is exactly the algebraic specification
above evaluated at machine precision; its factor is obtained from the
actual positive definite leading block. No jitter, alternate start, SDP,
repair, grid, precision rerun or second optimization is permitted.

Initial abort tests: finite real arrays of the correct dimensions; full
flow, Me, direct-array/chart comparison and full factor residual <=1e-9;
all30 coefficients>=epsilon(1-1e-6); full minimum eigenvalue>=-1e-9;
complement minimum>=1/900-1e-9; leading minimum>=1/5400-1e-9; all search
boxes and factor diagonal signs. Failure aborts without optimization.

Numerical candidate tests: actual original coefficient signs >=0;
full flow and Me infinity norms<=1e-7; full original/factor residual<=1e-7;
full minimum eigenvalue>=-1e-8; original/model matrix discrepancy<=1e-8;
factor diagonals>=0 and all search boxes. Stop early once such a point has
A>Gamma5+1e-6. All negative entries and eigenvalues remain visible. This
is only numerical credibility, never exact PSD or a certificate proof.
No exact reconstruction is planned in this job: complete arrays are saved
for a separately selected next stage if the candidate test succeeds.

Counters: EvaluationMonitor increments an exact callback counter and
throws at2000. StepMonitor increments an exact step counter and performs
one full ORIGINAL-model readback at every accepted step. It throws by598
steps, reserving two of the600 total readbacks for initial and final checks.
The solver's internal iterations also have MaxIterations600. The initial
and returned/final point are checked separately; the best checked point
is retained with its already computed readback, avoiding a redundant call.

Time: one30s charge made before evaluation, no refund. Model build and
optimizer phase has a23s kernel guard with a22s callback wall guard; full
readback and compressed export have an overall26.5s kernel guard and the
evaluator receives timeConstraint27. Reserve3s for local decoding and
persistence, enforced by a local SIGALRM. The core deadline is absolute
from call start, not renewed per iteration. Accounted cap is30 regardless
of early exit; actual kernel, transport wall, and local processing times
are recorded separately. Unknown-time timeout charges the full30 and
closes the ledger. The prior870s conservative reservation becomes900s;
neither number is represented as measured runtime.

Output: final full readback FIRST, then best feasible and initial arrays;
h,Lambda,M,C,JC,J and a fixed orthonormal complement basis; all original
flows, Me, factor and model residuals, signs and full/complement/leading
eigenvalues; counters, solver output, version, status and every received
warning. Objective history is explicitly SAMPLED: step1,every10th step,
and the final readback. Exact total and omitted counts are supplied.
There is no per-callback full-vector dump. The complete last callback
vector is retained as a trial point, without an unperformed readback.
Lossless gzip/base64 chunks preserve the complete bounded payload; raw
received response is saved before parsing. No clipping, truncation,
rounding-away of negative values, or reconstructed missing output.

## 5. Retrieval and authority limits

The shared COMPLETE draft841a70f2ad83841d was read in full and its SHA256
matched4e1f0afaa542bb54c0c4e047e72f9a50df6e60011718bd2a1305b8ffa6f4c6b6.
The derivation above independently reproduces the needed formulas from
accepted originals; the shared draft is not promoted to a fact.
The known exact local BnB-PEP paper2203.07305v5 was read at Lemma3 and
Algorithm1 Stage2 for the factor/interior-point method analogy. No external
theorem is imported. Matlas hits on generalized eigenvalues/nonnegative
rank were unrelated. Official Wolfram FindMinimum and TimeConstrained
documentation was checked. No branch-and-bound or global optimum claim
is made. All new algebra remains unverified because the current task
expressly forbids fact_submit. Failure of this run cannot prove full-cone
nonexistence or settle the original problem.


## Retained file: run.wl

Block[{$MessageList = {}}, Module[
 {started = AbsoluteTime[], h, nm, nv, la, cv, cm, vv, path, tree,
  off, pairs, cuts, ee, tt, zz, om, lf, dd, mm, bb, jj, qq, ff,
  cut, outside, aa, bt, den, uedge, vedge, eq, cons, starts,
  a0, b0, t0, delta0, x0, y0, s0, cap0, d0, cc0, kk0, tau0,
  eta0, u0, v0, eps0, h0, n0, direct, z0, sumz0, w0, gam0,
  initial = {}, initialRead = Null, finalRead = Null, bestRead = Null,
  finalVector = {}, bestA = -Infinity, lastStep = {}, lastEval = {},
  lastRead = Null, iter = 0, evals = 0, reads = 0, calls = 0,
  status = "building", stopReason = "not_started", initialPassed = False,
  initialChecks = <||>, directError = Null, sol = Null, ruleSol = {},
  history = {}, maxTrialCapacity = Null, phaseResult, result, payload,
  chunks, encoded, read, onEval, onStep, row, realVectorQ,
  tol = 10^-7, psdtol = 10^-8, gamma = 7977607/806000,
  tag = "max3equalityguard", kernelElapsed, preExportElapsed,
  modelBuildElapsed = Null, initialElapsed = Null, optimizationElapsed = Null,
  optimizationStarted = Null, exportStarted, elapsedAtRead,
  exactAttempted = False},
 TimeConstrained[
  phaseResult = Catch[TimeConstrained[
   ee = ConstantArray[1, 6];
   path = {1, 2, 3, 6, 5, 4};
   tree = Partition[path, 2, 1];
   cuts = Table[Take[path, k], {k, 5}];
   pairs = Select[Tuples[Range[6], 2], #[[1]] != #[[2]] &];
   off = Select[pairs, ! MemberQ[Sort /@ tree, Sort[#]] &];
   h = Table[Unique["step"], {5}];
   nv = Table[Unique["offtree"], {20}];
   nm = ConstantArray[0, {6, 6}];
   Do[nm[[off[[k, 1]], off[[k, 2]]]] = nv[[k]], {k, 20}];
   cm = Table[If[j <= i, Unique["factor"], 0], {i, 5}, {j, 5}];
   cv = Flatten[Table[cm[[i, j]], {i, 5}, {j, i}]];
   vv = Join[h, nv, cv];
   jj = Join[IdentityMatrix[5], {-ConstantArray[1, 5]}];
   qq = N[Table[Which[i <= j, 1/Sqrt[j (j + 1)],
           i == j + 1, -j/Sqrt[j (j + 1)], True, 0], {i, 6}, {j, 5}]];
   tt = Table[Which[i == j, 1, j < i, h[[j]], True, 0], {i, 6}, {j, 6}];
   zz = tt.ee; om = Join[-2 h, {2 Total[h]}]; la = nm;
   Do[
    {uedge, vedge} = tree[[k]]; cut = cuts[[k]];
    outside = Complement[Range[6], cut];
    aa = Total[om[[cut]]] - Total[Flatten[Table[nm[[i, j]] - nm[[j, i]],
                                                {i, cut}, {j, outside}]]];
    bt = -Total[Flatten[Table[nm[[i, j]] zz[[j]] - nm[[j, i]] zz[[i]],
                                                {i, cut}, {j, outside}]]];
    den = zz[[uedge]] - zz[[vedge]];
    la[[uedge, vedge]] = (zz[[uedge]] aa - bt)/den;
    la[[vedge, uedge]] = (zz[[vedge]] aa - bt)/den,
    {k, 5}];
   lf = DiagonalMatrix[la.ee] - la;
   dd = DiagonalMatrix[Join[h (2 - h), {-Total[h] (Total[h] + 2)}]];
   mm = dd + (lf.tt + Transpose[tt].Transpose[lf])/2;
   bb = mm[[1 ;; 5, 1 ;; 5]]; ff = jj.cm;
   eq = Flatten[Table[(bb - cm.Transpose[cm])[[i, j]], {i, 5}, {j, i}]];
   cons = DeleteCases[Join[
     Thread[eq == 0], Thread[h >= 1/4], Thread[h <= 6],
     Thread[Extract[la, pairs] >= 0], Thread[Extract[la, pairs] <= 400],
     Thread[cv >= -100], Thread[cv <= 100], Thread[Diagonal[cm] >= 0],
     Thread[Flatten[ff] >= -100], Thread[Flatten[ff] <= 100]], True];
   modelBuildElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;

   realVectorQ[v_List] := VectorQ[v, NumericQ] &&
      FreeQ[v, Indeterminate | _DirectedInfinity | _Complex];
   read[values_List, initialFactor_: False] := Module[
    {rr, vUsed = values, hv, lm, cf, tm, fl, omega, dm, mat, sym,
     basisFactor, flow, null, fr, mr, pe, ce, ev, eqr, lv, box,

    reads++;
    If[reads > 600, status = "readback_cap"; Throw[$Failed, tag]];
    If[! realVectorQ[values] || Length[values] != 40,
      Return[<|"finite_real" -> False, "numerically_feasible" -> False,
        "variables_input" -> ToString[values, InputForm]|>]];
    rr = Thread[vv -> values]; hv = h /. rr; lm = N[la /. rr];
    tm = Table[Which[i == j, 1., j < i, hv[[j]], True, 0.], {i, 6}, {j, 6}];
    fl = DiagonalMatrix[lm.ee] - lm;
    omega = Join[-2 hv, {2 Total[hv]}];
    dm = DiagonalMatrix[Join[hv (2 - hv), {-Total[hv] (Total[hv] + 2)}]];
    mat = dm + (fl.tm + Transpose[tm].Transpose[fl])/2;
    finite = realVectorQ[Flatten[mat]] && realVectorQ[Flatten[lm]];
    If[! finite, Return[<|"finite_real" -> False, "h" -> hv,
       "Lambda_text" -> ToString[lm, InputForm], "M_text" -> ToString[mat, InputForm],
       "numerically_feasible" -> False|>]];
    ev = Sort[Eigenvalues[mat]];
    pe = Sort[Eigenvalues[mat[[1 ;; 5, 1 ;; 5]]]];
    ce = Sort[Eigenvalues[Transpose[qq].mat.qq]];
    cf = cm /. rr;
    If[TrueQ[initialFactor] && Min[pe] > 0,
     cf = Transpose[CholeskyDecomposition[mat[[1 ;; 5, 1 ;; 5]]]];
     vUsed = Join[Take[values, 25], Flatten[Table[cf[[i, j]], {i, 5}, {j, i}]]];
     rr = Thread[vv -> vUsed]];
    basisFactor = jj.cf;
    flow = Transpose[fl].ee - omega; null = mat.ee;
    fr = mat - basisFactor.Transpose[basisFactor];
    mr = (mm /. rr) - mat; eqr = eq /. rr;
    lv = Extract[lm, pairs]; fdiag = Diagonal[cf];
    box = Min[hv] >= 1/4 && Max[hv] <= 6 && Min[lv] >= 0 && Max[lv] <= 400 &&
          Max[Abs[Flatten[cf]]] <= 100 && Max[Abs[Flatten[basisFactor]]] <= 100;
    diagok = Min[fdiag] >= 0;
    me = Max[Abs[flow]]; ne = Max[Abs[null]];
    fe = Max[Abs[Flatten[fr]]]; mre = Max[Abs[Flatten[mr]]];
    feas = TrueQ[box && diagok && me <= tol && ne <= tol && fe <= tol &&
                  Min[ev] >= -psdtol && mre <= psdtol];
    <|"h" -> hv, "A" -> 1 + Total[hv], "Lambda" -> lm, "M" -> mat,
      "C" -> cf, "full_factor" -> basisFactor, "variables" -> vUsed,
      "T" -> tm, "omega" -> omega,
      "flow_residual" -> flow, "null_residual" -> null,
      "full_factor_residual" -> fr, "model_minus_original_residual" -> mr,
      "factor_equalities" -> eqr,
      "full_eigenvalues" -> ev, "complement_eigenvalues" -> ce,
      "principal_eigenvalues" -> pe, "offdiagonal_signs" -> Thread[lv >= 0],
      "factor_diagonal_signs" -> Thread[fdiag >= 0],
      "min_original_coefficient" -> Min[lv], "max_original_coefficient" -> Max[lv],
      "min_step" -> Min[hv], "max_step" -> Max[hv],
      "max_flow_residual" -> me, "max_null_residual" -> ne,
      "max_factor_residual" -> fe, "max_model_original_residual" -> mre,
      "max_factor_equality_residual" -> Max[Abs[eqr]],
      "all_search_boxes" -> TrueQ[box], "finite_real" -> finite,
      "numerically_feasible" -> feas,
      "exceeds_exact_threshold_numerically" -> TrueQ[1 + Total[hv] > gamma],
      "credible_target" -> TrueQ[feas && 1 + Total[hv] > gamma + 10^-6]|>];

   a0 = N[Sqrt[2]]; b0 = (-a0 + Sqrt[10 + 8 a0])/2;
   t0 = N[99/100]; delta0 = N[1/100]; eps0 = N[1/10000000];

   d0 = (a0 - 1) cap0 - delta0;
   cc0 = (cap0 + d0)/(cap0 - d0); kk0 = 1 + 2 cc0;

   z0 = {1., 1 + x0, cap0, cap0 + 1 + d0, 2 cap0 + d0 - y0, 2 cap0 + d0};
   sumz0 = 2 + cap0 + 3 (2 cap0 + d0) - 2 y0;
   w0 = {(sumz0 - 6)/x0, (2 sumz0 - 6 (2 + x0))/y0,

   Do[direct[[off[[i, 1]], off[[i, 2]]]] += eps0, {i, 20}];

      direct[[tree[[i, 2]], tree[[i, 1]]]] += eps0 gam0[[i]], {i, 5}];

     "all_numerical_feasibility_checks" -> TrueQ[initialRead["numerically_feasible"]],

     "full_psd_check" -> TrueQ[Min[initialRead["full_eigenvalues"]] >= -10^-9],
     "complement_margin" -> TrueQ[Min[initialRead["complement_eigenvalues"]] >= 1/900 - 10^-9],
     "leading_margin" -> TrueQ[Min[initialRead["principal_eigenvalues"]] >= 1/5400 - 10^-9],
     "flow_tight" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_flow_residual"] <= 10^-9],
     "null_tight" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_null_residual"] <= 10^-9],
     "factor_tight" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_factor_residual"] <= 10^-9],
     "chart_direct_array_match" -> TrueQ[directError <= 10^-9]|>;
   initialPassed = And @@ Values[initialChecks]; initialElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;
   lastStep = initial; lastRead = initialRead;
   If[! TrueQ[initialPassed], status = "initial_original_readback_failed_abort";
     stopReason = status; Throw[$Failed, tag]];
   bestRead = initialRead; bestA = initialRead["A"]; maxTrialCapacity = bestA;
   row[ir_, er_, rr_] := {ir, er, AbsoluteTime[] - started, rr["A"],
     rr["min_original_coefficient"], rr["max_flow_residual"], rr["max_null_residual"],
     Min[rr["full_eigenvalues"]], rr["max_factor_residual"], rr["numerically_feasible"]};
   onEval[] := (
     evals++; lastEval = vv;
     maxTrialCapacity = Max[maxTrialCapacity, 1 + Total[h]];
     If[evals >= 2000, status = "evaluation_callback_cap"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "callback_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]]);
   onStep[] := Module[{point = vv, rr},
     iter++;
     If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "step_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     rr = read[point]; lastStep = point; lastRead = rr;
     If[! TrueQ[rr["finite_real"]], status = "nonfinite_step_abort"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[TrueQ[rr["numerically_feasible"]] && rr["A"] > bestA,
        bestRead = rr; bestA = rr["A"]];
     If[iter == 1 || Mod[iter, 10] == 0, AppendTo[history, row[iter, evals, rr]]];
     If[TrueQ[rr["credible_target"]], status = "credible_original_candidate"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[iter >= 598 || reads >= 599, status = "step_readback_cap"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "step_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]]];
   starts = Transpose[{vv, initial}];
   If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "build_initial_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
   status = "optimizing"; calls = 1; optimizationStarted = AbsoluteTime[];
   sol = Catch[TimeConstrained[
     FindMinimum[Evaluate[{-(1 + Total[h]), cons}], Evaluate[starts],
       Method -> "InteriorPoint", WorkingPrecision -> MachinePrecision,
       AccuracyGoal -> 8, PrecisionGoal -> 8, MaxIterations -> 600,
       EvaluationMonitor :> onEval[], StepMonitor :> onStep[]],
     Max[0.01, 23 - (AbsoluteTime[] - started)],
     status = "optimizer_time_guard"; $Aborted], tag];
   optimizationElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - optimizationStarted;
   If[status == "optimizing", status = If[MatchQ[sol, {_?NumericQ, _List}],
       "optimizer_returned_no_convergence_asserted", "optimizer_no_numeric_return"]];
   stopReason = status;
   , Max[0.01, 23 - (AbsoluteTime[] - started)],
     status = "model_and_optimizer_time_guard"; stopReason = status; $Aborted], tag];

  If[stopReason == "not_started", stopReason = status];
  If[TrueQ[initialPassed] && calls == 1,
   If[MatchQ[sol, {_?NumericQ, _List}], ruleSol = Last[sol]; finalVector = vv /. ruleSol,
     finalVector = lastStep];
   If[realVectorQ[finalVector] && Length[finalVector] == 40 && reads < 600,
      finalRead = read[finalVector], finalRead = lastRead];
   If[TrueQ[finalRead["numerically_feasible"]] && finalRead["A"] > bestA,
      bestRead = finalRead; bestA = finalRead["A"]];
   If[TrueQ[bestRead["credible_target"]], status = "credible_original_candidate"],
   finalRead = initialRead];
  preExportElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;
  result = <|
    "final_readback" -> finalRead,
    "best_feasible_readback" -> bestRead,
    "initial_readback" -> initialRead,
    "J_full_factor_basis" -> jj, "orthonormal_complement_basis" -> qq,
    "initial_direct_Lambda" -> direct, "initial_direct_chart_max_error" -> directError,
    "initial_checks" -> initialChecks, "initial_passed" -> initialPassed,
    "status" -> status, "optimizer_stop_reason" -> stopReason, "version" -> $Version,
    "method" -> "InteriorPoint", "accuracy_goal" -> 8, "precision_goal" -> 8,
    "original_readback_tolerance" -> tol, "numerical_psd_tolerance" -> psdtol,
    "exact_threshold" -> ToString[gamma, InputForm], "accounted_seconds" -> 30,
    "optimizer_invocations" -> calls, "evaluation_callback_count" -> evals,
    "step_monitor_count" -> iter, "original_readback_count" -> reads,
    "max_optimizer_iterations" -> 600, "callback_cap" -> 2000, "readback_cap" -> 600,
    "variable_count" -> Length[vv], "factor_equality_count" -> Length[eq],
    "offtree_pairs_original_zero_based" -> (off - 1),
    "solver_result_inputform" -> ToString[sol, InputForm],
    "kernel_messages" -> ToString[$MessageList, InputForm],
    "sampled_history_scope" -> "StepMonitor first and every tenth; final row separate. No evaluation trace stored; total callback count exact.",
    "sampled_history_columns" -> {"step", "callback", "seconds", "capacity", "min_Lambda",
       "flow_inf", "null_inf", "full_min_eigenvalue", "factor_inf", "numerically_feasible"},
    "sampled_history" -> history, "sampled_step_rows" -> Length[history],
    "unsampled_step_count" -> iter - Length[history],
    "final_history_row" -> If[TrueQ[initialPassed] && AssociationQ[finalRead] &&
                  TrueQ[finalRead["finite_real"]], row[iter, evals, finalRead], Null],
    "maximum_trial_capacity_not_feasibility_claim" -> maxTrialCapacity,
    "last_evaluation_vector_unchecked_if_distinct_from_final" -> lastEval,
    "exact_reconstruction_attempted" -> exactAttempted,
    "model_build_seconds" -> modelBuildElapsed,
    "initial_checks_complete_seconds_from_start" -> initialElapsed,
    "optimizer_seconds" -> optimizationElapsed,
    "kernel_seconds_before_export" -> preExportElapsed|>;
  exportStarted = AbsoluteTime[];
  payload = BaseEncode[ExportByteArray[ExportString[result, "RawJSON", "Compact" -> True], {"GZIP", "Text"}]];
  chunks = StringPartition[payload, UpTo[3000]];
  Do[Print["EQ45_CHUNK_", k, "_OF_", Length[chunks], "=", chunks[[k]]], {k, Length[chunks]}];
  kernelElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;
  ExportString[<|"status" -> status, "initial_passed" -> initialPassed,
      "best_feasible_A" -> If[bestRead === Null, Null, bestA],
      "optimizer_invocations" -> calls, "evaluation_callback_count" -> evals,
      "step_monitor_count" -> iter, "original_readback_count" -> reads,
      "export_chunks" -> Length[chunks], "encoding" -> "base64-gzip-UTF8-JSON",
      "kernel_seconds_including_payload_export" -> kernelElapsed,
      "payload_export_seconds" -> AbsoluteTime[] - exportStarted,
      "accounted_seconds" -> 30|>, "RawJSON", "Compact" -> True],
  26.5,
  ExportString[<|"status" -> "overall_kernel_timeout", "initial_passed" -> initialPassed,
      "optimizer_invocations" -> calls, "evaluation_callback_count" -> evals,
      "step_monitor_count" -> iter, "original_readback_count" -> reads,
      "kernel_messages" -> ToString[$MessageList, InputForm],
      "kernel_seconds" -> AbsoluteTime[] - started, "accounted_seconds" -> 30,
      "complete_arrays_available" -> False|>, "RawJSON", "Compact" -> True]]
]]

# One full equality-chart search: derivation and frozen plan

Status: joint UNVERIFIED research derivation and numerical-discovery plan.
Owner max3, continuation round after completed44, ceiling45/attempted46.
Authority: current TASK.md and master guidance59c43473b63b1939. Exactly
ONE mathematical job,30 conservatively charged seconds; no retry, formal
gate, installation, consult, native agent, or automatic renewal.

The original exact-profile Goal, W=2R, and the sufficient threshold in
accepted4c92d3fc4eb6153e are unchanged. The numerical target is an actual
length5 word with A>Gamma5=7977607/806000, preferably A>=99/10. A numerical
endpoint is only a candidate. No seed or original-target theorem is asserted.

## 1. Correctness inputs and reproduced equality chart

The accepted inputs are04219554e41d9750, Section10, and1252b8823bb5a290,
proof paragraph1. They give the original finite interpolation orientation
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0 and the certificate identity

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_5)>,
 V_h=2sum_(i<5)h_i(f_i-f_5)+sum_(i<5)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_5||^2,
 s=sum h_i, A=1+s,
 T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
 L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_4,2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<5,-s(s+2)), M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2,

provided L^T e=omega. All six gradients are independent formal variables,
e is the six-vector of ones, and Lambda has zero diagonal. All30 original
ordered off-diagonal coefficients must be nonnegative, and M must be PSD
on the whole R^6. The coefficient identity is also checked by separating
position terms sym(L(T-I)) and squared-gradient terms sym(L)-diag(omega)/2.
Adding the diagonal part of V_h yields exactly D+sym(LT).

Put z=Te, Z=diag(z), d(X)=Xe-X^Te, and d_z(X)=Xz-ZX^Te=d(XZ).
Both divergences have total sum zero. Direct coordinate calculation gives
2De+T^T omega=-Z omega: if p_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, its i<5 coordinate is
2h_i(2-h_i)-2h_i+2h_i(p_i+h_i)=2h_i(1+p_i)=-z_i omega_i.
The last coordinate is -2s(s+2)+2s=-2s(s+1).
Thus, when d(Lambda)=omega,

 2Me=Lz-Zomega=-d_z(Lambda).

Consequently the two original equalities are equivalent to
d(Lambda)=omega and d_z(Lambda)=0. Moreover, under the first equality,
e^TMe=0 because e^TDe=-(s^2+sum h_i^2) and z^Tomega=s^2+sum h_i^2.
If M is PSD, the quadratic (e+tv)^TM(e+tv) has zero constant term;
nonnegativity for both signs of t forces e^TMv=0 for all v. Hence Me=0
is necessary for every original feasible certificate and costs no solutions.

Use the path v=(0,1,2,5,4,3), oriented successively. For edge u->w let
S be the initial path segment through u, Delta=z_u-z_w, and N have all20
independent off-tree ordered entries, zero diagonal and tree entries.
Define

 alpha=sum_(i in S)omega_i-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij-N_ji),
 beta=-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij z_j-N_ji z_i).
 Lambda_uw=(z_u alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_wu=(z_w alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_ij=N_ij off the tree.

This solves a-b=alpha and z_w a-z_u b=beta. Every tree cut of each
residual divergence is zero. Rooting the tree, a leaf residual is zero
by its singleton subtree cut; subtracting zero child subtree sums proves
each parent residual zero, and the root follows from total sum zero.
Conversely every original equality array must satisfy these two cut
equations, whose determinant is -Delta, so its dependent entries are
uniquely the displayed formulas. The inverse just reads the20 off-tree
entries. They and all five h_i remain independent variables.

The successive cuts are {0},{0,1},{0,1,2},{0,1,2,5},{0,1,2,5,4}; their
Delta values are -h_0,-h_1,-(h_2+h_3+h_4),h_4,h_3. They are nonzero in
the registered box. This is a coordinate basis, not a support assumption.
At a zero Delta the actual compatibility condition is beta=z_u alpha;
if compatible the whole pair is (t+alpha,t), otherwise no completion
exists at those off-tree coordinates. These singular cases lie outside
this search box and are not declared infeasible for the original problem.
In particular a box restriction is not a global theorem about the cone.

## 2. Full-space factor equivalence, including singular factors

Write B for the leading5-by-5 block of M and J=[I_5;-1_5^T]. From Me=0
one obtains all final row and column entries, giving M=JBJ^T. For arbitrary
independent g_0,...,g_5 its quadratic form is
(g_(0:4)-g_5 1)^TB(g_(0:4)-g_5 1). It follows in both directions that
M>=0 iff B>=0. This identity does not identify any two gradients.

A symmetric PSD B has a lower triangular factor C with nonnegative
diagonal, B=CC^T, including the singular case. Induct on the first pivot
of the current PSD residual. A positive pivot p allows a square completion
and leaves the PSD Schur residual. If p=0, positivity of each two-variable
quadratic forces its whole off-diagonal row zero; set that factor column
zero and continue on the remaining principal submatrix. A negative pivot,
or zero pivot with nonzero off-diagonal row, is incompatible with PSD.
Conversely any CC^T is PSD. This establishes exact equivalence without
fixing rank. The full factor is F=JC; its last row is minus the sum of
the first five rows. All15 lower factor entries vary in the search.

## 3. Reproducing the specified strict start, without adopting a draft fact

Let a=sqrt2, rho=a-1, lambda=1+a,
b=(-a+sqrt(10+8a))/2, p=a+b, t=99/100, delta=1/100,
X=ta, Y=tb, S=X+Y, C0=1+S, d=rho C0-delta,
h_*=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y), A_*=2C0+d,
c=(C0+d)/(C0-d), k=1+2c,
tau=delta(2a C0-delta)/(C0-d), eta=2tau,
u=2S(1+X)/Y, v0=2SC0/Y, epsilon=1/10000000.
Here C0 is a scalar capacity, C above is a matrix factor, and v0 is a
scalar coefficient, distinct from the path v.
The identity b(a+b)=2(1+a) follows by squaring the defining root.

The two-step child (X,Y) has multipliers Lambda_01=2,
Lambda_10=2(1+X), Lambda_12=u, Lambda_21=v0, other entries zero.
Its flow is (-2X,-2Y,2S), since v0-u=2S. Direct substitution gives the
path-Laplacian remainder with edge weights

 w0=2-X^2=2delta(1+t),
 w1=(S/Y)[2-Y^2+(2-Y)X]=2(p/b)delta(1+lambda t).

For two such children and the bridge1+d, the complete baseline array is

 [0,       2,       0,       0,          0,         0]
 [2(1+X),  0,       u,       0,          0,         0]
 [0,       v0,      0,       0,          0,         2cd+eta]
 [0,       0,       2cX,     0,          2k,        2cX]
 [0,       0,       2cY,     2k(1+X),    0,         ku+2cY]
 [0,       0,       2c+eta,  0,          kv0,       0].

Its last four divergences are respectively
v0-u+2c(d-C0)=-2(1+d), 4cX-2kX=-2X,
4cY+2kX+k(u-v0)=-2Y, k(v0-u)+2c(2-C0-d)=2s.
The first two are the child divergences. Thus all six flows are checked.

Here is a full quadratic check of its remainder. Write
U_L=g0-g1, V_L=g1-g2, U_R=g3-g4, V_R=g4-g5,
zeta=g2-g5, Kvec=X U_R+S V_R.
Expanding the original matrix, using c(C0-d)=C0+d and
d(2C0+d)-C0^2=-delta(2a C0-delta), gives

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
       +k(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
       +tau[(C0-d)||zeta||^2-2<zeta,Kvec>].

One way to check every mixed term is to use
E=XQ_32+YQ_42+Q_52+dQ_25+XQ_35+YQ_45.
The tail-to-tail terms cancel between its two tail sums; bridge-to-tail
terms cancel against dQ_25; tail-to-terminal terms against Q_52; the
bridge-to-terminal terms in Q_52 and dQ_25 cancel. Its remaining gradient
part is X(1-X)||g3||^2+Y(1-Y)||g4||^2+
[(C0-d)/2-d^2]||g2||^2+(C0+d)||g5||^2/2.
The identity V_h=V_left+kV_right-2cE+
c[d(2C0+d)-C0^2](||g5||^2-||g2||^2) follows by matching its values and
diagonals. Adding eta(Q_25+Q_52), with
Q_25+Q_52=-d||zeta||^2-(C0+d)<zeta,g5>-<zeta,Kvec>, gives exactly the
displayed M0[g]. Thus the full matrix, rather than only its diagonal,
has been reproduced.

Put R=X^2/w0+S^2/w1=Rbar/delta,
Rbar=t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)], q=tau/(C0-d), sigma=k-qR,
E0=zeta-Kvec/(C0-d). Square completion gives the exact identity

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
 +sigma(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
 +q||S sqrt(w0/w1)U_R-X sqrt(w1/w0)V_R||^2
 +tau(C0-d)||E0||^2.

The cross term in the fourth square is -2qXS<U_R,V_R>, and its other
terms equal q[R(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)-||Kvec||^2], verifying the
identity. For quantitative bounds, the increasing polynomial
F(x)=x^2+ax-2(1+a) changes sign between8/5 and161/100, while
7/5<a<71/50. Hence3<p<303/100,397/100<C0<403/100,
1<X<71/50,1<Y<161/100,3/2<d<17/10,39/100<d/C0<21/50,
(29/50)C0<C0-d<(61/100)C0<3.
Both terms in Rbar increase for t>=0, so Rbar<=lambda/2.
Consequently k>339/61>11/2 and
qR<(7/2)C0/[(29/50)^2 C0^2]=8750/(841C0)<3, giving sigma>5/2.
Also w0,w1>3delta, sigma w0,sigma w1>(15/2)delta, and
tau(C0-d)>11delta. The bounds X/(C0-d)<2/3 and S/(C0-d)<4/3 imply
||zeta||^2<=3||E0||^2+(4/3)||U_R||^2+(16/3)||V_R||^2.
Therefore M0[g]>=delta E_tree, where E_tree is the sum of the five

along that path gives edge coefficients15,24,27,24,15, hence

where P=I-ee^T/6. This proves the baseline margin directly.

To fill every missing multiplier, let K0=6I-ee^T and
F_l=sum_(i in S_l)(K0z)_i, w_l=F_l/(z_u-z_w), gamma_l=1-w_l.
Set H=K0-sum_l w_l(e_u-e_w)(e_u-e_w)^T. Then He=0.
Also Hz=0: at vertex v_l the edge sum has coordinate F_l-F_(l-1),

# Equality-chart round45: initial readback failed before optimization

The single authorized job is CLOSED. No optimizer ran, no sufficient seed
was found, and the original Goal remains OPEN. This is an implementation
failure at the initial factorization/readback gate, not a local optimum,
subthreshold search result, or mathematical nonexistence result.

## What was prepared

The registered plan832803668bb414af reproduces the chart and the specific
dense start jointly from accepted04219554e41d9750/1252b8823bb5a290. It
retains every original coefficient and all six formal gradients, with
five variable steps,20 off-tree coefficients, and15 lower-factor entries.
The full-space PSD equivalence, including singular factors, and the
strict-point algebra are written out in model_and_plan.md. All new
algebra remains UNVERIFIED. Shared841a70f2ad83841d was discovery input,
not an accepted predecessor. The exact threshold remains the one in
accepted4c92d3fc4eb6153e; its actual-seed hypothesis was not fulfilled.

The plan, executable source, and accounting parser were hashed before
any mathematical evaluation. Registration and charge preceded the only
Wolfram evaluator invocation. There was no calibration, warm-start
solve, second start, precision rerun, reconstruction, formal gate,
installation, consult, or native subagent.

## Observed result, including failures

The complete raw received text begins with:

 CholeskyDecomposition::herm: The matrix {{0.039800532882957707,
 -0.0397997975148926, -4. ... 361, -0.23081230000691022,
 0.9712896894104067}} is not Hermitian or real and symmetric.

The displayed ellipsis was already in the actual tool message. It is
not a recovered full matrix. Part::partw, Thread::tdlen, and
Diagonal::list messages followed because the unevaluated factorization
expression propagated. The exporter then reported:

 Export::jsonstrictencoding: Expression "Transpose" cannot be exported
 as "JSON".

The tool's separate footer, decoded literally from its returned string,
is complete and records:

 status: initial_original_readback_failed_abort
 initial_passed: false
 best_feasible_A: null
 optimizer_invocations: 0
 evaluation_callback_count: 0
 step_monitor_count: 0
 original_readback_count: 1
 export_chunks: 1
 kernel_seconds_including_payload_export: 0.582564
 payload_export_seconds: 0.318138
 accounted_seconds: 30

The one announced gzip/base64 chunk was received completely. It decodes
to the seven-byte literal `$Failed`, not to the intended JSON object.
The local decoder consequently returned exactly
`JSONDecodeError('Expecting value: line 1 column 1 (char 0)')`.
This original decoder outcome is retained in
outcome_initial_decoder_failure.json. Administrative inspection of the
already received bytes recovered the footer and preserved the failed
payload; it did not evaluate a mathematical model or rerun the job.

No complete initial/final h,Lambda,M or factor arrays, residual vectors,
or spectra survived serialization. Therefore the numerical strict start
was NOT successfully checked. Neither original feasibility nor failure
of PSD is established by this execution. There are no optimizer steps,
callbacks, or objective history to interpret. The exact start remains
specified algebraically in the registered source and proof draft; it
has not been recomputed to replace the missing observed arrays.

The reason for Cholesky's symmetry rejection is not independently
established from the retained output. The mathematical expression is
symmetric. The source computes its two transpose-related products
separately in floating point, which is a possible source of tiny
asymmetry; that explanation is an inference, not a measured residual or
a verified diagnosis. No repair call was made. The source also lacked
a numeric-factor validation before export, so the first failure caused
a second failure of output serialization. Both limitations are retained.

## Accounting and closure

The ledger is equality_chart_local_search_20261001_budget.json, status

Exactly1 mathematical job and30 accounted seconds were charged, without

# Equality-chart repair: complete evidence and closed allocation

The separately allocated implementation repair passed every original initial
check and reached its ONE authorized optimizer invocation. It did not produce
a sufficient-capacity seed. The best originally checked numerical capacity was
9.694142701216034, below Gamma5=7977607/806000. The original exact-profile goal
remains OPEN. There is no new accepted fact or exact feasibility claim.

solver convergence return: status `invalid_original_step_readback_abort`, solver
return `$Aborted`. The final original two-product evaluation had a tiny complex
conjugate eigenvalue pair. Every complete initial/final original numerical array
and every received message was retained. The ledger is CLOSED with no retry.

source byte for byte. The replacement matrix formula is symbolically identical:
for P=LT, P^T=T^T L^T, so D+(P+P^T)/2 is the specified original M. Mirroring its
upper triangle merely represents that exact symmetry. The full proof and all
unchanged variables, signs, boxes and tolerances are in repair_plan.md and the
copied original_model_and_plan.md. This is a working implementation derivation,
not verifier acceptance of a new mathematical fact.

At the original dense start, the independently evaluated former two-product
formula had maximum asymmetry3.552713678800501e-15. Its entrywise difference

These new observed arrays and successful factorization support a floating

## Exact symmetric formula: direct algebra, not a feasibility repair

Set P=LT ONCE. For all indices i,j,
 (P^T)_ij=P_ji=sum_k L_jk T_ki
               =sum_k (T^T)_ik (L^T)_kj=(T^T L^T)_ij.
Therefore D+(P+P^T)/2 is identically the specified original matrix M.
Since D is diagonal, its (i,j) and (j,i) entries agree exactly. Retaining
the upper entry and copying it into the lower position implements this
identity: it does not average an arbitrary nonsymmetric matrix, change
a diagonal, project to the PSD cone, or alter any multiplier.

The new source constructs the symbolic model and every numerical readback
from one product P and its literal transpose. It also separately evaluates
M_old=D+(P+T^T L^T)/2 by the former two-product order, retaining BOTH full
arrays. Each readback includes M_old-M, M_old-M_old^T, M-M^T, the difference
between the unmirrored one-product formula and the mirrored representation,
and the full spectrum of M_old. This is a numerical discrepancy diagnostic,
not a claim about unseen arrays from the failed old job. A tiny new
M_old-M discrepancy together with successful factorization would support
floating evaluation asymmetry as the implementation explanation; it could
not recover or prove the actual old job's unobserved matrix.

## Pre-factor evidence and failure behavior

The new readback first validates h and Lambda dimensions and finite-real
entries, then assembles all original matrices. BEFORE Cholesky it saves and
emits a separate JSON-safe compressed stream EQ46_INITIAL containing h,
Lambda,T,L,D,P,M,M_old, the unmirrored one-product matrix, all original flow
and null residuals, symmetry and discrepancy arrays, full/leading/complement
spectra, the old-form spectrum, all30 coefficient signs and original boxes.
A later factorization or final encoding failure cannot erase this emitted
snapshot. If an earlier invalid array or spectral result is encountered,
available values are retained with explicit error tags and no optimization.

Original prechecks must pass before factoring: flow and both null residuals
<=1e-9, assembly discrepancy<=1e-9, zero bitwise symmetry/mirroring residual,
all30 coefficients>=epsilon(1-1e-6), original step/coefficient boxes, minimum
full eigenvalue>=-1e-9, leading>=1/5400-1e-9 and complement>=1/900-1e-9.
These retain the previous criteria and add the independent assembly guards.
Cholesky uses TargetStructure->Dense solely to specify its return container.
Check catches a factorization message as $Failed. Its result must be a real
finite5-by-5 matrix before any transpose, entry extraction, Thread or Diagonal
on that result. Failure stops cleanly with its tagged output. The original
full-factor residual<=1e-9 and direct/chart-array error<=1e-9 remain required
before optimizer initialization. No tolerance is weakened.

Every export passes a recursive JSON conversion that retains strings, lists,
associations, finite ordinary real numbers, booleans and Null. Exact rational
metadata are tagged with their exact InputForm; every other symbolic/failure/
nonfinite expression is explicitly tagged with its InputForm. No unsupported

succeeds, a literal plain-JSON fallback is emitted. A footer is printed as

received messages and the full encoded response are saved before decoding;

## Unchanged optimizer and explicit stopping rules

If every initial check passes, run at most ONE FindMinimum on -(1+sum h),

<=1e-8, nonnegative factor diagonals and every search box. Added tests require

is never exact certification. No rational reconstruction is planned in this

Counters stop by2000 EvaluationMonitor callbacks,598 StepMonitor callbacks,

# One full equality-chart search: derivation and frozen plan

Status: joint UNVERIFIED research derivation and numerical-discovery plan.
Owner max3, continuation round after completed44, ceiling45/attempted46.
Authority: current TASK.md and master guidance59c43473b63b1939. Exactly
ONE mathematical job,30 conservatively charged seconds; no retry, formal
gate, installation, consult, native agent, or automatic renewal.

The original exact-profile Goal, W=2R, and the sufficient threshold in
accepted4c92d3fc4eb6153e are unchanged. The numerical target is an actual
length5 word with A>Gamma5=7977607/806000, preferably A>=99/10. A numerical
endpoint is only a candidate. No seed or original-target theorem is asserted.

## 1. Correctness inputs and reproduced equality chart

The accepted inputs are04219554e41d9750, Section10, and1252b8823bb5a290,
proof paragraph1. They give the original finite interpolation orientation
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0 and the certificate identity

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_5)>,
 V_h=2sum_(i<5)h_i(f_i-f_5)+sum_(i<5)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_5||^2,
 s=sum h_i, A=1+s,
 T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
 L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_4,2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<5,-s(s+2)), M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2,

provided L^T e=omega. All six gradients are independent formal variables,
e is the six-vector of ones, and Lambda has zero diagonal. All30 original
ordered off-diagonal coefficients must be nonnegative, and M must be PSD
on the whole R^6. The coefficient identity is also checked by separating
position terms sym(L(T-I)) and squared-gradient terms sym(L)-diag(omega)/2.
Adding the diagonal part of V_h yields exactly D+sym(LT).

Put z=Te, Z=diag(z), d(X)=Xe-X^Te, and d_z(X)=Xz-ZX^Te=d(XZ).
Both divergences have total sum zero. Direct coordinate calculation gives
2De+T^T omega=-Z omega: if p_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, its i<5 coordinate is
2h_i(2-h_i)-2h_i+2h_i(p_i+h_i)=2h_i(1+p_i)=-z_i omega_i.
The last coordinate is -2s(s+2)+2s=-2s(s+1).
Thus, when d(Lambda)=omega,

 2Me=Lz-Zomega=-d_z(Lambda).

Consequently the two original equalities are equivalent to
d(Lambda)=omega and d_z(Lambda)=0. Moreover, under the first equality,
e^TMe=0 because e^TDe=-(s^2+sum h_i^2) and z^Tomega=s^2+sum h_i^2.
If M is PSD, the quadratic (e+tv)^TM(e+tv) has zero constant term;
nonnegativity for both signs of t forces e^TMv=0 for all v. Hence Me=0
is necessary for every original feasible certificate and costs no solutions.

Use the path v=(0,1,2,5,4,3), oriented successively. For edge u->w let
S be the initial path segment through u, Delta=z_u-z_w, and N have all20
independent off-tree ordered entries, zero diagonal and tree entries.
Define

 alpha=sum_(i in S)omega_i-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij-N_ji),
 beta=-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij z_j-N_ji z_i).
 Lambda_uw=(z_u alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_wu=(z_w alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_ij=N_ij off the tree.

This solves a-b=alpha and z_w a-z_u b=beta. Every tree cut of each
residual divergence is zero. Rooting the tree, a leaf residual is zero
by its singleton subtree cut; subtracting zero child subtree sums proves
each parent residual zero, and the root follows from total sum zero.
Conversely every original equality array must satisfy these two cut
equations, whose determinant is -Delta, so its dependent entries are
uniquely the displayed formulas. The inverse just reads the20 off-tree
entries. They and all five h_i remain independent variables.

The successive cuts are {0},{0,1},{0,1,2},{0,1,2,5},{0,1,2,5,4}; their
Delta values are -h_0,-h_1,-(h_2+h_3+h_4),h_4,h_3. They are nonzero in
the registered box. This is a coordinate basis, not a support assumption.
At a zero Delta the actual compatibility condition is beta=z_u alpha;
if compatible the whole pair is (t+alpha,t), otherwise no completion
exists at those off-tree coordinates. These singular cases lie outside
this search box and are not declared infeasible for the original problem.
In particular a box restriction is not a global theorem about the cone.

## 2. Full-space factor equivalence, including singular factors

Write B for the leading5-by-5 block of M and J=[I_5;-1_5^T]. From Me=0
one obtains all final row and column entries, giving M=JBJ^T. For arbitrary
independent g_0,...,g_5 its quadratic form is
(g_(0:4)-g_5 1)^TB(g_(0:4)-g_5 1). It follows in both directions that
M>=0 iff B>=0. This identity does not identify any two gradients.

A symmetric PSD B has a lower triangular factor C with nonnegative
diagonal, B=CC^T, including the singular case. Induct on the first pivot
of the current PSD residual. A positive pivot p allows a square completion
and leaves the PSD Schur residual. If p=0, positivity of each two-variable
quadratic forces its whole off-diagonal row zero; set that factor column
zero and continue on the remaining principal submatrix. A negative pivot,
or zero pivot with nonzero off-diagonal row, is incompatible with PSD.
Conversely any CC^T is PSD. This establishes exact equivalence without
fixing rank. The full factor is F=JC; its last row is minus the sum of
the first five rows. All15 lower factor entries vary in the search.

## 3. Reproducing the specified strict start, without adopting a draft fact

Let a=sqrt2, rho=a-1, lambda=1+a,
b=(-a+sqrt(10+8a))/2, p=a+b, t=99/100, delta=1/100,
X=ta, Y=tb, S=X+Y, C0=1+S, d=rho C0-delta,
h_*=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y), A_*=2C0+d,
c=(C0+d)/(C0-d), k=1+2c,
tau=delta(2a C0-delta)/(C0-d), eta=2tau,
u=2S(1+X)/Y, v0=2SC0/Y, epsilon=1/10000000.
Here C0 is a scalar capacity, C above is a matrix factor, and v0 is a
scalar coefficient, distinct from the path v.
The identity b(a+b)=2(1+a) follows by squaring the defining root.

The two-step child (X,Y) has multipliers Lambda_01=2,
Lambda_10=2(1+X), Lambda_12=u, Lambda_21=v0, other entries zero.
Its flow is (-2X,-2Y,2S), since v0-u=2S. Direct substitution gives the
path-Laplacian remainder with edge weights

 w0=2-X^2=2delta(1+t),
 w1=(S/Y)[2-Y^2+(2-Y)X]=2(p/b)delta(1+lambda t).

For two such children and the bridge1+d, the complete baseline array is

 [0,       2,       0,       0,          0,         0]
 [2(1+X),  0,       u,       0,          0,         0]
 [0,       v0,      0,       0,          0,         2cd+eta]
 [0,       0,       2cX,     0,          2k,        2cX]
 [0,       0,       2cY,     2k(1+X),    0,         ku+2cY]
 [0,       0,       2c+eta,  0,          kv0,       0].

Its last four divergences are respectively
v0-u+2c(d-C0)=-2(1+d), 4cX-2kX=-2X,
4cY+2kX+k(u-v0)=-2Y, k(v0-u)+2c(2-C0-d)=2s.
The first two are the child divergences. Thus all six flows are checked.

Here is a full quadratic check of its remainder. Write
U_L=g0-g1, V_L=g1-g2, U_R=g3-g4, V_R=g4-g5,
zeta=g2-g5, Kvec=X U_R+S V_R.
Expanding the original matrix, using c(C0-d)=C0+d and
d(2C0+d)-C0^2=-delta(2a C0-delta), gives

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
       +k(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
       +tau[(C0-d)||zeta||^2-2<zeta,Kvec>].

One way to check every mixed term is to use
E=XQ_32+YQ_42+Q_52+dQ_25+XQ_35+YQ_45.
The tail-to-tail terms cancel between its two tail sums; bridge-to-tail
terms cancel against dQ_25; tail-to-terminal terms against Q_52; the
bridge-to-terminal terms in Q_52 and dQ_25 cancel. Its remaining gradient
part is X(1-X)||g3||^2+Y(1-Y)||g4||^2+
[(C0-d)/2-d^2]||g2||^2+(C0+d)||g5||^2/2.
The identity V_h=V_left+kV_right-2cE+
c[d(2C0+d)-C0^2](||g5||^2-||g2||^2) follows by matching its values and
diagonals. Adding eta(Q_25+Q_52), with
Q_25+Q_52=-d||zeta||^2-(C0+d)<zeta,g5>-<zeta,Kvec>, gives exactly the
displayed M0[g]. Thus the full matrix, rather than only its diagonal,
has been reproduced.

Put R=X^2/w0+S^2/w1=Rbar/delta,
Rbar=t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)], q=tau/(C0-d), sigma=k-qR,
E0=zeta-Kvec/(C0-d). Square completion gives the exact identity

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
 +sigma(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
 +q||S sqrt(w0/w1)U_R-X sqrt(w1/w0)V_R||^2
 +tau(C0-d)||E0||^2.

The cross term in the fourth square is -2qXS<U_R,V_R>, and its other
terms equal q[R(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)-||Kvec||^2], verifying the
identity. For quantitative bounds, the increasing polynomial
F(x)=x^2+ax-2(1+a) changes sign between8/5 and161/100, while
7/5<a<71/50. Hence3<p<303/100,397/100<C0<403/100,
1<X<71/50,1<Y<161/100,3/2<d<17/10,39/100<d/C0<21/50,
(29/50)C0<C0-d<(61/100)C0<3.
Both terms in Rbar increase for t>=0, so Rbar<=lambda/2.
Consequently k>339/61>11/2 and

## 4. Frozen numerical specification

Implementation: run.wl, executed ONCE by the existing Wolfram evaluator.
All machine mathematics, including model construction, initial arrays,
factorization, optimization, original readbacks, and encoding, occurs in
that single call. No code is evaluated for calibration beforehand.

Variables: five independent h; all20 off-tree ordered coefficients in
lexicographic order; all15 entries of a lower triangular5-by-5 factor C.
The ten dependent Lambda entries use the exact cuts above. No reflection,
support, repeated row, zero remainder, fixed rank, or one-parameter path
is imposed. The relation h=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y) holds ONLY at the initial point.
Equality constraints are the15 lower triangular entries of B-CC^T=0.
All30 multiplier inequalities remain imposed, including tree entries.

Search box:1/4<=each h_i<=6;0<=each original Lambda_ij<=400 (i!=j);
-100<=each lower factor entry<=100, diagonal>=0; additionally every entry
of the FULL6-by-5 factor JC is in[-100,100]. These are search restrictions,
not proved global bounds. The registered objective is maximize1+sum h_i.

Method: one FindMinimum call on its negative, Method->InteriorPoint,
WorkingPrecision->MachinePrecision, AccuracyGoal->8, PrecisionGoal->8,
MaxIterations->600. Initial point is exactly the algebraic specification
above evaluated at machine precision; its factor is obtained from the
actual positive definite leading block. No jitter, alternate start, SDP,
repair, grid, precision rerun or second optimization is permitted.

Initial abort tests: finite real arrays of the correct dimensions; full
flow, Me, direct-array/chart comparison and full factor residual <=1e-9;
all30 coefficients>=epsilon(1-1e-6); full minimum eigenvalue>=-1e-9;
complement minimum>=1/900-1e-9; leading minimum>=1/5400-1e-9; all search
boxes and factor diagonal signs. Failure aborts without optimization.

Numerical candidate tests: actual original coefficient signs >=0;
full flow and Me infinity norms<=1e-7; full original/factor residual<=1e-7;
full minimum eigenvalue>=-1e-8; original/model matrix discrepancy<=1e-8;
factor diagonals>=0 and all search boxes. Stop early once such a point has
A>Gamma5+1e-6. All negative entries and eigenvalues remain visible. This
is only numerical credibility, never exact PSD or a certificate proof.
No exact reconstruction is planned in this job: complete arrays are saved
for a separately selected next stage if the candidate test succeeds.

Counters: EvaluationMonitor increments an exact callback counter and
throws at2000. StepMonitor increments an exact step counter and performs
one full ORIGINAL-model readback at every accepted step. It throws by598
steps, reserving two of the600 total readbacks for initial and final checks.
The solver's internal iterations also have MaxIterations600. The initial
and returned/final point are checked separately; the best checked point
is retained with its already computed readback, avoiding a redundant call.

Time: one30s charge made before evaluation, no refund. Model build and
optimizer phase has a23s kernel guard with a22s callback wall guard; full
readback and compressed export have an overall26.5s kernel guard and the
evaluator receives timeConstraint27. Reserve3s for local decoding and
persistence, enforced by a local SIGALRM. The core deadline is absolute
from call start, not renewed per iteration. Accounted cap is30 regardless
of early exit; actual kernel, transport wall, and local processing times
are recorded separately. Unknown-time timeout charges the full30 and
closes the ledger. The prior870s conservative reservation becomes900s;
neither number is represented as measured runtime.

Output: final full readback FIRST, then best feasible and initial arrays;
h,Lambda,M,C,JC,J and a fixed orthonormal complement basis; all original
flows, Me, factor and model residuals, signs and full/complement/leading
eigenvalues; counters, solver output, version, status and every received
warning. Objective history is explicitly SAMPLED: step1,every10th step,
and the final readback. Exact total and omitted counts are supplied.
There is no per-callback full-vector dump. The complete last callback
vector is retained as a trial point, without an unperformed readback.
Lossless gzip/base64 chunks preserve the complete bounded payload; raw
received response is saved before parsing. No clipping, truncation,
rounding-away of negative values, or reconstructed missing output.

## 5. Retrieval and authority limits

matched4e1f0afaa542bb54c0c4e047e72f9a50df6e60011718bd2a1305b8ffa6f4c6b6.
The derivation above independently reproduces the needed formulas from

The known exact local BnB-PEP paper2203.07305v5 was read at Lemma3 and
Algorithm1 Stage2 for the factor/interior-point method analogy. No external
theorem is imported. Matlas hits on generalized eigenvalues/nonnegative
rank were unrelated. Official Wolfram FindMinimum and TimeConstrained
documentation was checked. No branch-and-bound or global optimum claim
is made. All new algebra remains unverified because the current task
expressly forbids fact_submit. Failure of this run cannot prove full-cone
nonexistence or settle the original problem.

   x0 = t0 a0; y0 = t0 b0; s0 = x0 + y0; cap0 = 1 + s0;
   d0 = (a0 - 1) cap0 - delta0;
   cc0 = (cap0 + d0)/(cap0 - d0); kk0 = 1 + 2 cc0;
   tau0 = delta0 (2 a0 cap0 - delta0)/(cap0 - d0); eta0 = 2 tau0;
   u0 = 2 s0 (1 + x0)/y0; v0 = 2 s0 cap0/y0;
   h0 = {x0, y0, 1 + d0, x0, y0};
   n0 = Table[Which[MemberQ[{{4, 3}, {4, 6}}, off[[i]]], 2 cc0 x0 + eps0,
                   off[[i]] == {5, 3}, 2 cc0 y0 + eps0, True, eps0], {i, 20}];
   z0 = {1., 1 + x0, cap0, cap0 + 1 + d0, 2 cap0 + d0 - y0, 2 cap0 + d0};
   sumz0 = 2 + cap0 + 3 (2 cap0 + d0) - 2 y0;
   w0 = {(sumz0 - 6)/x0, (2 sumz0 - 6 (2 + x0))/y0,
     (3 sumz0 - 6 (2 + x0 + cap0))/(cap0 + d0),
     (6 (2 + x0 + cap0 + 2 cap0 + d0) - 4 sumz0)/y0,
     (sumz0 - 6 (cap0 + 1 + d0))/x0};
   gam0 = 1 - w0;
   direct = {{0., 2, 0, 0, 0, 0}, {2 (1 + x0), 0, u0, 0, 0, 0},
    {0, v0, 0, 0, 0, 2 cc0 d0 + eta0},
    {0, 0, 2 cc0 x0, 0, 2 kk0, 2 cc0 x0},
    {0, 0, 2 cc0 y0, 2 kk0 (1 + x0), 0, kk0 u0 + 2 cc0 y0},
    {0, 0, 2 cc0 + eta0, 0, kk0 v0, 0}};
   Do[direct[[off[[i, 1]], off[[i, 2]]]] += eps0, {i, 20}];
   Do[direct[[tree[[i, 1]], tree[[i, 2]]]] += eps0 gam0[[i]];
      direct[[tree[[i, 2]], tree[[i, 1]]]] += eps0 gam0[[i]], {i, 5}];
   initialRead = read[Join[h0, n0, ConstantArray[0., 15]], True];
   If[! TrueQ[initialRead["finite_real"]],
     status = "initial_nonfinite_abort"; stopReason = status; Throw[$Failed, tag]];
   If[! TrueQ[Lookup[initialRead, "factor_valid", False]],
     status = "initial_factor_or_original_checks_abort"; stopReason = status;
     Throw[$Failed, tag]];
   initial = initialRead["variables"];
   directError = Max[Abs[Flatten[initialRead["Lambda"] - direct]]];
   initialChecks = <|
     "all_numerical_feasibility_checks" -> TrueQ[initialRead["numerically_feasible"]],
     "dense_coefficient_margin" -> TrueQ[initialRead["min_original_coefficient"] >= eps0 (1 - 10^-6)],
     "full_psd_check" -> TrueQ[Min[initialRead["full_eigenvalues"]] >= -10^-9],
     "complement_margin" -> TrueQ[Min[initialRead["complement_eigenvalues"]] >= 1/900 - 10^-9],
     "leading_margin" -> TrueQ[Min[initialRead["principal_eigenvalues"]] >= 1/5400 - 10^-9],
     "flow_tight" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_flow_residual"] <= 10^-9],
     "null_tight" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_null_residual"] <= 10^-9],
     "factor_tight" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_factor_residual"] <= 10^-9],
     "chart_direct_array_match" -> TrueQ[directError <= 10^-9],
     "original_assembly_agreement" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_original_assembly_discrepancy"] <= 10^-9],
     "literal_symmetric_formula" -> TrueQ[initialRead["max_symmetry_residual"] == 0 &&
         initialRead["max_single_product_mirror_discrepancy"] == 0],
     "pre_factor_snapshot_exported" -> initialSnapshotExported,
     "factor_shape_and_finite_entries" -> factorValid|>;
   initialPassed = And @@ Values[initialChecks]; initialElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;
   lastStep = initial; lastRead = initialRead;
   If[! TrueQ[initialPassed], status = "initial_original_readback_failed_abort";
     stopReason = status; Throw[$Failed, tag]];
   bestRead = initialRead; bestA = initialRead["A"]; maxTrialCapacity = bestA;
   row[ir_, er_, rr_] := {ir, er, AbsoluteTime[] - started, rr["A"],
     rr["min_original_coefficient"], rr["max_flow_residual"], rr["max_null_residual"],
     Min[rr["full_eigenvalues"]], rr["max_factor_residual"], rr["numerically_feasible"]};
   onEval[] := (
     evals++; lastEval = vv;
     If[! realVectorQ[lastEval] || Length[lastEval] != 40,
       status = "nonnumeric_evaluation_callback_abort"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     maxTrialCapacity = Max[maxTrialCapacity, 1 + Total[h]];
     If[evals >= 2000, status = "evaluation_callback_cap"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "callback_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]]);
   onStep[] := Module[{point = vv, rr},
     iter++;
     If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "step_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     rr = read[point]; lastStep = point; lastRead = rr;
     If[! TrueQ[rr["finite_real"]] || ! TrueQ[Lookup[rr, "factor_valid", False]],
       status = "invalid_original_step_readback_abort"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[TrueQ[rr["numerically_feasible"]] && rr["A"] > bestA,
        bestRead = rr; bestA = rr["A"]];
     If[iter == 1 || Mod[iter, 10] == 0, AppendTo[history, row[iter, evals, rr]]];
     If[TrueQ[rr["credible_target"]], status = "credible_original_candidate"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[iter >= 598 || reads >= 599, status = "step_readback_cap"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
     If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "step_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]]];
   starts = Transpose[{vv, initial}];
   If[AbsoluteTime[] - started >= 22, status = "build_initial_wall_guard"; Throw[$Aborted, tag]];
   status = "optimizing"; calls = 1; optimizationStarted = AbsoluteTime[];
   sol = Catch[TimeConstrained[
     FindMinimum[Evaluate[{-(1 + Total[h]), cons}], Evaluate[starts],
       Method -> "InteriorPoint", WorkingPrecision -> MachinePrecision,
       AccuracyGoal -> 8, PrecisionGoal -> 8, MaxIterations -> 600,
       EvaluationMonitor :> onEval[], StepMonitor :> onStep[]],
     Max[0.01, 23 - (AbsoluteTime[] - started)],
     status = "optimizer_time_guard"; $Aborted], tag];
   optimizationElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - optimizationStarted;
   If[status == "optimizing", status = If[MatchQ[sol, {_?NumericQ, _List}],
       "optimizer_returned_no_convergence_asserted", "optimizer_no_numeric_return"]];
   stopReason = status;
   , Max[0.01, 23 - (AbsoluteTime[] - started)],
     status = "model_and_optimizer_time_guard"; stopReason = status; $Aborted], tag];

  If[stopReason == "not_started", stopReason = status];
  If[TrueQ[initialPassed] && calls == 1,
   If[MatchQ[sol, {_?NumericQ, _List}], ruleSol = Last[sol]; finalVector = vv /. ruleSol,
     finalVector = lastStep];
   If[realVectorQ[finalVector] && Length[finalVector] == 40 && reads < 600,
      finalRead = read[finalVector], finalRead = lastRead];
   If[TrueQ[finalRead["numerically_feasible"]] && finalRead["A"] > bestA,
      bestRead = finalRead; bestA = finalRead["A"]];
   If[TrueQ[bestRead["credible_target"]], status = "credible_original_candidate"],
   finalRead = initialRead];
  preExportElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;
  result = <|
    "final_readback" -> finalRead,
    "best_feasible_readback" -> bestRead,
    "initial_readback" -> initialRead,
    "initial_pre_factor_snapshot" -> initialSnapshot,
    "initial_pre_factor_snapshot_exported" -> initialSnapshotExported,
    "factorization_status" -> factorizationStatus,
    "factorization_output" -> factorizationOutput,
    "factor_shape_and_finite_entries_validated" -> factorValid,
    "J_full_factor_basis" -> jj, "orthonormal_complement_basis" -> qq,
    "initial_direct_Lambda" -> direct, "initial_direct_chart_max_error" -> directError,
    "initial_checks" -> initialChecks, "initial_passed" -> initialPassed,
    "status" -> status, "optimizer_stop_reason" -> stopReason, "version" -> $Version,
    "method" -> "InteriorPoint", "accuracy_goal" -> 8, "precision_goal" -> 8,
    "original_readback_tolerance" -> tol, "numerical_psd_tolerance" -> psdtol,
    "tolerances_exact_inputform" -> <|"original" -> ToString[tol, InputForm],
       "psd" -> ToString[psdtol, InputForm]|>,
    "exact_threshold" -> ToString[gamma, InputForm], "accounted_seconds" -> 30,
    "optimizer_invocations" -> calls, "evaluation_callback_count" -> evals,
    "step_monitor_count" -> iter, "original_readback_count" -> reads,
    "max_optimizer_iterations" -> 600, "callback_cap" -> 2000, "readback_cap" -> 600,
    "variable_count" -> Length[vv], "factor_equality_count" -> Length[eq],
    "offtree_pairs_original_zero_based" -> (off - 1),
    "solver_result_inputform" -> ToString[sol, InputForm],
    "kernel_messages" -> ToString[$MessageList, InputForm],
    "sampled_history_scope" -> "StepMonitor first and every tenth; final row separate. No evaluation trace stored; total callback count exact.",
    "sampled_history_columns" -> {"step", "callback", "seconds", "capacity", "min_Lambda",
       "flow_inf", "null_inf", "full_min_eigenvalue", "factor_inf", "numerically_feasible"},
    "sampled_history" -> history, "sampled_step_rows" -> Length[history],
    "all_original_readback_summaries" -> readbackLog,
    "original_readback_summaries_count" -> Length[readbackLog],
    "unsampled_step_count" -> iter - Length[history],
    "final_history_row" -> If[TrueQ[initialPassed] && AssociationQ[finalRead] &&
                  TrueQ[Lookup[finalRead, "factor_valid", False]], row[iter, evals, finalRead], Null],
    "maximum_trial_capacity_not_feasibility_claim" -> maxTrialCapacity,
    "last_evaluation_vector_unchecked_if_distinct_from_final" -> lastEval,
    "exact_reconstruction_attempted" -> exactAttempted,
    "model_build_seconds" -> modelBuildElapsed,
    "initial_checks_complete_seconds_from_start" -> initialElapsed,
    "optimizer_seconds" -> optimizationElapsed,
    "kernel_seconds_before_export" -> preExportElapsed|>;
  exportStarted = AbsoluteTime[];
  exportResult = emit["EQ46_FINAL", result];
  kernelElapsed = AbsoluteTime[] - started;
  Print["EQ46_FOOTER_JSON=", ExportString[jsonSafe[<|"record_type" -> "EQ46_FOOTER",
      "status" -> status, "initial_passed" -> initialPassed,
      "best_feasible_A" -> If[bestRead === Null, Null, bestA],
      "optimizer_invocations" -> calls, "evaluation_callback_count" -> evals,
      "step_monitor_count" -> iter, "original_readback_count" -> reads,
      "final_export" -> exportResult, "initial_export" -> snapshotExport,
      "factorization_status" -> factorizationStatus,
      "encoding" -> "base64-gzip-UTF8-JSON-with-tagged-non-JSON-values",
      "kernel_seconds_including_payload_export" -> kernelElapsed,
      "payload_export_seconds" -> AbsoluteTime[] - exportStarted,
      "accounted_seconds" -> 30|>], "RawJSON", "Compact" -> True]];
  "EQ46_COMPLETE",
  26.5,
  Print["EQ46_FOOTER_JSON=", ExportString[jsonSafe[<|"record_type" -> "EQ46_FOOTER",
      "status" -> "overall_kernel_timeout", "initial_passed" -> initialPassed,
      "optimizer_invocations" -> calls, "evaluation_callback_count" -> evals,
      "step_monitor_count" -> iter, "original_readback_count" -> reads,
      "initial_pre_factor_snapshot_exported" -> initialSnapshotExported,
      "factorization_status" -> factorizationStatus,
      "kernel_messages" -> ToString[$MessageList, InputForm],
      "kernel_seconds" -> AbsoluteTime[] - started, "accounted_seconds" -> 30,
      "complete_final_arrays_available" -> False|>], "RawJSON", "Compact" -> True]];
  "EQ46_TIMEOUT"]
]]
