SHA256 c3ccdccf6ad7c69817bfa5b4fffb79f6e6248b00111d0c9a68f4bacfcdff5142

```python
"""One new reservation and lossless persistence. No mathematical evaluation."""
from pathlib import Path
import base64
import datetime
import fcntl
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import re
import signal
import sys
import time

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
LEDGER = BASE.parent / 'equality_chart_repair_20261001_budget.json'


def utc():
    return datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()


def write(path, data):
    temp = path.with_name(path.name + '.tmp')
    with temp.open('w') as stream:
        json.dump(data, stream, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
        stream.write('\n')
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    os.replace(temp, path)


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def parse_objects(text):
    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
    for match in re.finditer(r'\{', text):
        try:
            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[match.start():])
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            continue
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            yield obj


def strings(value):
    if isinstance(value, str):
        yield value
    elif isinstance(value, dict):
        for v in value.values():
            yield from strings(v)
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for v in value:
            yield from strings(v)


def decode_stream(alltext, label, destination):
    """Always retain encoded/decoded bytes, including invalid JSON payloads."""
    info = {'label': label, 'complete': False, 'json_object': False}
    hits = re.findall(re.escape(label) + r'_CHUNK_(\d+)_OF_(\d+)=\s*([A-Za-z0-9+/=]+)', alltext)
    chunkmap = {}
    totals = set()
    try:
        for index, total, chunk in hits:
            k = int(index)
            if k in chunkmap and chunkmap[k] != chunk:
                raise ValueError('Conflicting duplicate chunk')
            chunkmap[k] = chunk
            totals.add(int(total))
        info['received_chunk_indices'] = sorted(chunkmap)
        info['announced_chunk_totals'] = sorted(totals)
        write(destination.with_suffix('.chunks.json'), {'chunks': chunkmap, 'totals': sorted(totals)})
        if len(totals) == 1 and set(chunkmap) == set(range(1, next(iter(totals)) + 1)):
            encoded = ''.join(chunkmap[i] for i in sorted(chunkmap))
            destination.with_suffix('.base64.txt').write_text(encoded)
            compressed = base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)
            destination.with_suffix('.received.gz').write_bytes(compressed)
            plain = gzip.decompress(compressed)
            destination.with_suffix('.received.txt').write_bytes(plain)
            info['complete'] = True
            info['payload_sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(plain).hexdigest()
            obj = json.loads(plain)
        else:
            marker = label + '_JSON_FALLBACK='
            if marker not in alltext:
                return None, info
            tail = alltext.split(marker, 1)[1]
            obj, end = json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode(tail.lstrip())
            destination.with_suffix('.received.txt').write_text(tail.lstrip()[:end])
            info['complete'] = True
            info['fallback_plain_JSON'] = True
        if not isinstance(obj, dict):
            info['error'] = {'tag': 'non_object_JSON', 'received_type': type(obj).__name__}
            return None, info
        info['json_object'] = True
        write(destination.with_suffix('.json'), obj)
        return obj, info
    except Exception as error:
        info['error'] = {'tag': 'decode_error', 'detail': repr(error)}
        return None, info


def real(x):
    return isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool) and math.isfinite(x)


def vector(x, n):
    return isinstance(x, list) and len(x) == n and all(real(a) for a in x)


def matrix(x, m, n):
    return isinstance(x, list) and len(x) == m and all(vector(row, n) for row in x)


def original_arrays_available(r):
    return isinstance(r, dict) and vector(r.get('h'), 5) and matrix(r.get('Lambda'), 6, 6) \
        and matrix(r.get('M'), 6, 6) and matrix(r.get('M_original_two_product'), 6, 6) \
        and vector(r.get('flow_residual'), 6) and vector(r.get('null_residual'), 6)


def compact_readback(r):
    if not isinstance(r, dict):
        return r
    keys = ['h', 'A', 'min_original_coefficient', 'max_original_coefficient',
            'max_flow_residual', 'max_null_residual', 'max_factor_residual',
            'max_model_original_residual', 'max_factor_equality_residual',
            'full_eigenvalues', 'complement_eigenvalues', 'principal_eigenvalues',
            'original_two_product_eigenvalues', 'max_original_assembly_discrepancy',
            'max_single_product_mirror_discrepancy', 'max_symmetry_residual',
            'max_original_two_product_symmetry_residual',
            'max_original_two_product_null_residual', 'factorization_status',
            'factor_valid', 'numerically_feasible', 'credible_target', 'all_search_boxes', 'error']
    return {k: r[k] for k in keys if k in r}


def reserve():
    ledger = json.loads(LEDGER.read_text())
    manifest = json.loads((BASE / 'manifest.json').read_text())
    assert ledger['status'] == 'allocated_not_started'
    assert ledger['charged_jobs_used'] == 0 and ledger['accounted_seconds_used'] == 0
    assert ledger['in_flight'] is None and ledger['charged_job_limit'] == 1
    assert ledger['accounted_seconds_limit'] == 30
    assert not (BASE / 'reservation.json').exists()
    for name, expected in manifest['source_sha256'].items():
        assert digest(BASE / name) == expected, 'Source changed after registration: ' + name
    receipt = {
        'job_name': 'equality_chart_symmetric_assembly_single_repair',
        'status': 'reserved_charged', 'reserved_utc': utc(),
        'accounted_seconds': 30, 'measured_seconds': None,
        'source_sha256': manifest['source_sha256'],
        'manifest_sha256': digest(BASE / 'manifest.json'),
        'optimizer_invocation_limit': 1, 'kernel_time_constraint': 27,
        'local_postprocessing_guard_seconds': 3, 'no_retry': True,
        'old_failed_job_remains_charged': 30,
    }
    ledger.update(status='active_single_repair_reserved', charged_jobs_used=1,
                  accounted_seconds_used=30, in_flight=receipt['job_name'])
    ledger['jobs'].append(receipt)
    ledger['reservation_plus_accounted_seconds'] = 930
    write(LEDGER, ledger)
    write(BASE / 'reservation.json', receipt)
    print(json.dumps(receipt))


def finish():
    began = time.perf_counter()
    save_seconds = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0.0
    outcome = {'status': 'closed_unknown_output', 'raw_file': 'raw_received.json',
               'accounted_seconds': 30, 'local_raw_save_seconds': save_seconds,
               'complete_initial_original_arrays_available': False,
               'complete_final_original_arrays_available': False,
               'no_retry': True, 'formal_calls': 0, 'new_accepted_facts': 0}
    result = initial = footer = None

    def alarm(*_):
        raise TimeoutError('Local persistence/decode guard expired')

    signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, alarm)
    try:
        signal.setitimer(signal.ITIMER_REAL, max(0.001, 2.5 - save_seconds))
        raw = json.loads((BASE / 'raw_received.json').read_text())
        outcome['transport_wall_seconds'] = raw.get('transport_wall_seconds')
        texts = list(strings(raw.get('tool_result', raw)))
        alltext = '\n'.join(texts)
        (BASE / 'raw_text_received.txt').write_text(alltext)
        for obj in parse_objects(alltext):
            if obj.get('record_type') == 'EQ46_FOOTER':
                footer = obj
        if footer is not None:
            write(BASE / 'footer.json', footer)
            outcome['footer'] = footer
            outcome['measured_kernel_seconds'] = footer.get(
                'kernel_seconds_including_payload_export', footer.get('kernel_seconds'))
            for key in ('initial_passed', 'optimizer_invocations', 'evaluation_callback_count',
                        'step_monitor_count', 'original_readback_count', 'factorization_status'):
                if key in footer:
                    outcome[key] = footer[key]
        initial, initial_info = decode_stream(alltext, 'EQ46_INITIAL', BASE / 'initial_snapshot')
        result, final_info = decode_stream(alltext, 'EQ46_FINAL', BASE / 'result')
        outcome['initial_stream'] = initial_info
        outcome['final_stream'] = final_info
        outcome['all_export_chunks_received'] = initial_info['complete'] and final_info['complete']
        outcome['complete_initial_original_arrays_available'] = original_arrays_available(initial)
        outcome['initial_pre_factor_summary'] = compact_readback(initial)
        if result is not None:
            for key in ('status', 'optimizer_stop_reason', 'initial_passed', 'initial_checks',
                        'initial_direct_chart_max_error', 'optimizer_invocations',
                        'evaluation_callback_count', 'step_monitor_count', 'original_readback_count',
                        'original_readback_summaries_count', 'factorization_status',
                        'factor_shape_and_finite_entries_validated', 'initial_pre_factor_snapshot_exported',
                        'kernel_messages', 'model_build_seconds',
                        'initial_checks_complete_seconds_from_start', 'optimizer_seconds',
                        'kernel_seconds_before_export', 'sampled_step_rows', 'unsampled_step_count',
                        'maximum_trial_capacity_not_feasibility_claim', 'exact_reconstruction_attempted'):
                if key in result:
                    outcome['optimizer_status' if key == 'status' else key] = result[key]
            final = result.get('final_readback')
            outcome['complete_final_original_arrays_available'] = original_arrays_available(final)
            outcome['initial_summary'] = compact_readback(result.get('initial_readback'))
            outcome['final_summary'] = compact_readback(final)
            outcome['best_feasible_summary'] = compact_readback(result.get('best_feasible_readback'))
            best = result.get('best_feasible_readback')
            candidate = isinstance(best, dict) and best.get('credible_target') is True
            if candidate:
                outcome['status'] = 'closed_credible_numerical_candidate'
            elif result.get('initial_passed') is not True:
                outcome['status'] = 'closed_initial_original_or_factor_failure'
            else:
                outcome['status'] = 'closed_no_credible_seed'
        elif initial is not None:
            outcome['status'] = 'closed_incomplete_final_initial_snapshot_preserved'
        else:
            outcome['status'] = 'closed_transport_or_encoding_failure'
        outcome['raw_sha256'] = digest(BASE / 'raw_received.json')
        outcome['raw_warning_prefix'] = alltext.split('EQ46_INITIAL_CHUNK_', 1)[0]
    except Exception as error:
        outcome['status'] = 'closed_output_processing_error'
        outcome['processing_error'] = {'tag': 'exception', 'detail': repr(error)}
    finally:
        signal.setitimer(signal.ITIMER_REAL, 0)
        outcome['local_decode_persist_seconds_before_closure'] = time.perf_counter() - began
        outcome['local_raw_save_plus_decode_seconds_before_closure'] = save_seconds + time.perf_counter() - began
        write(BASE / 'outcome.json', outcome)
        ledger = json.loads(LEDGER.read_text())
        ledger['status'] = outcome['status']
        ledger['in_flight'] = None
        ledger['closed_utc'] = utc()
        ledger['jobs'][0].update(status=outcome['status'], finished_utc=ledger['closed_utc'],
                                 outcome_file=str(BASE / 'outcome.json'), outcome=outcome)
        ledger['measured_kernel_seconds_used'] = outcome.get('measured_kernel_seconds')
        ledger['measured_seconds_used'] = outcome.get('measured_kernel_seconds')
        ledger['optimizer_callbacks_used'] = outcome.get('evaluation_callback_count')
        ledger['original_readbacks_used'] = outcome.get('original_readback_count')
        ledger['optimizer_invocations_used'] = outcome.get('optimizer_invocations')
        ledger['iterations_observed'] = outcome.get('step_monitor_count')
        ledger['external_timeout_seconds_charged'] = 30 if outcome.get('measured_kernel_seconds') is None else 0
        ledger['unused_jobs'] = 0
        ledger['unused_accounted_seconds'] = 0
        ledger['no_renewal_inferred'] = True
        write(LEDGER, ledger)
        elapsed = save_seconds + time.perf_counter() - began
        write(BASE / 'local_postprocess_receipt.json', {
            'raw_save_plus_decode_and_ledger_close_seconds': elapsed,
            'local_3_second_cap_met': elapsed <= 3,
            'scope': 'excludes only writing this small receipt and stdout emission'})
    print(json.dumps(outcome, allow_nan=False))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    with (BASE / 'ledger.lock').open('a') as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        if sys.argv[1] == 'reserve':
            reserve()
        elif sys.argv[1] == 'finish':
            finish()
        else:
            raise ValueError('Unknown action')
```

# One full equality-chart search: derivation and frozen plan

Status: joint UNVERIFIED research derivation and numerical-discovery plan.
Owner max3, continuation round after completed44, ceiling45/attempted46.
Authority: current TASK.md and master guidance59c43473b63b1939. Exactly
ONE mathematical job,30 conservatively charged seconds; no retry, formal
gate, installation, consult, native agent, or automatic renewal.

The original exact-profile Goal, W=2R, and the sufficient threshold in
accepted4c92d3fc4eb6153e are unchanged. The numerical target is an actual
length5 word with A>Gamma5=7977607/806000, preferably A>=99/10. A numerical
endpoint is only a candidate. No seed or original-target theorem is asserted.

## 1. Correctness inputs and reproduced equality chart

The accepted inputs are04219554e41d9750, Section10, and1252b8823bb5a290,
proof paragraph1. They give the original finite interpolation orientation
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0 and the certificate identity

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_5)>,
 V_h=2sum_(i<5)h_i(f_i-f_5)+sum_(i<5)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_5||^2,

 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_4,2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<5,-s(s+2)), M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2,

provided L^T e=omega. All six gradients are independent formal variables,
e is the six-vector of ones, and Lambda has zero diagonal. All30 original
ordered off-diagonal coefficients must be nonnegative, and M must be PSD
on the whole R^6. The coefficient identity is also checked by separating
position terms sym(L(T-I)) and squared-gradient terms sym(L)-diag(omega)/2.
Adding the diagonal part of V_h yields exactly D+sym(LT).

Put z=Te, Z=diag(z), d(X)=Xe-X^Te, and d_z(X)=Xz-ZX^Te=d(XZ).
Both divergences have total sum zero. Direct coordinate calculation gives
2De+T^T omega=-Z omega: if p_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, its i<5 coordinate is
2h_i(2-h_i)-2h_i+2h_i(p_i+h_i)=2h_i(1+p_i)=-z_i omega_i.
The last coordinate is -2s(s+2)+2s=-2s(s+1).
Thus, when d(Lambda)=omega,

 2Me=Lz-Zomega=-d_z(Lambda).

Consequently the two original equalities are equivalent to
d(Lambda)=omega and d_z(Lambda)=0. Moreover, under the first equality,
e^TMe=0 because e^TDe=-(s^2+sum h_i^2) and z^Tomega=s^2+sum h_i^2.
If M is PSD, the quadratic (e+tv)^TM(e+tv) has zero constant term;
nonnegativity for both signs of t forces e^TMv=0 for all v. Hence Me=0
is necessary for every original feasible certificate and costs no solutions.

Use the path v=(0,1,2,5,4,3), oriented successively. For edge u->w let
S be the initial path segment through u, Delta=z_u-z_w, and N have all20
independent off-tree ordered entries, zero diagonal and tree entries.
Define

 alpha=sum_(i in S)omega_i-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij-N_ji),
 beta=-sum_(i in S,j outside S)(N_ij z_j-N_ji z_i).
 Lambda_uw=(z_u alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_wu=(z_w alpha-beta)/Delta,
 Lambda_ij=N_ij off the tree.

This solves a-b=alpha and z_w a-z_u b=beta. Every tree cut of each
residual divergence is zero. Rooting the tree, a leaf residual is zero
by its singleton subtree cut; subtracting zero child subtree sums proves
each parent residual zero, and the root follows from total sum zero.
Conversely every original equality array must satisfy these two cut
equations, whose determinant is -Delta, so its dependent entries are
uniquely the displayed formulas. The inverse just reads the20 off-tree
entries. They and all five h_i remain independent variables.

The successive cuts are {0},{0,1},{0,1,2},{0,1,2,5},{0,1,2,5,4}; their
Delta values are -h_0,-h_1,-(h_2+h_3+h_4),h_4,h_3. They are nonzero in
the registered box. This is a coordinate basis, not a support assumption.
At a zero Delta the actual compatibility condition is beta=z_u alpha;
if compatible the whole pair is (t+alpha,t), otherwise no completion
exists at those off-tree coordinates. These singular cases lie outside
this search box and are not declared infeasible for the original problem.
In particular a box restriction is not a global theorem about the cone.

## 2. Full-space factor equivalence, including singular factors

Write B for the leading5-by-5 block of M and J=[I_5;-1_5^T]. From Me=0
one obtains all final row and column entries, giving M=JBJ^T. For arbitrary
independent g_0,...,g_5 its quadratic form is
(g_(0:4)-g_5 1)^TB(g_(0:4)-g_5 1). It follows in both directions that
M>=0 iff B>=0. This identity does not identify any two gradients.

A symmetric PSD B has a lower triangular factor C with nonnegative
diagonal, B=CC^T, including the singular case. Induct on the first pivot
of the current PSD residual. A positive pivot p allows a square completion
and leaves the PSD Schur residual. If p=0, positivity of each two-variable
quadratic forces its whole off-diagonal row zero; set that factor column
zero and continue on the remaining principal submatrix. A negative pivot,
or zero pivot with nonzero off-diagonal row, is incompatible with PSD.
Conversely any CC^T is PSD. This establishes exact equivalence without
fixing rank. The full factor is F=JC; its last row is minus the sum of
the first five rows. All15 lower factor entries vary in the search.

## 3. Reproducing the specified strict start, without adopting a draft fact

Let a=sqrt2, rho=a-1, lambda=1+a,
b=(-a+sqrt(10+8a))/2, p=a+b, t=99/100, delta=1/100,

 w0=2-X^2=2delta(1+t),
 w1=(S/Y)[2-Y^2+(2-Y)X]=2(p/b)delta(1+lambda t).

 [0,       2,       0,       0,          0,         0]
 [2(1+X),  0,       u,       0,          0,         0]
 [0,       v0,      0,       0,          0,         2cd+eta]
 [0,       0,       2cX,     0,          2k,        2cX]
 [0,       0,       2cY,     2k(1+X),    0,         ku+2cY]
 [0,       0,       2c+eta,  0,          kv0,       0].

Its last four divergences are respectively

4cY+2kX+k(u-v0)=-2Y, k(v0-u)+2c(2-C0-d)=2s.
The first two are the child divergences. Thus all six flows are checked.

Here is a full quadratic check of its remainder. Write
U_L=g0-g1, V_L=g1-g2, U_R=g3-g4, V_R=g4-g5,
zeta=g2-g5, Kvec=X U_R+S V_R.
Expanding the original matrix, using c(C0-d)=C0+d and
d(2C0+d)-C0^2=-delta(2a C0-delta), gives

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
       +k(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
       +tau[(C0-d)||zeta||^2-2<zeta,Kvec>].

One way to check every mixed term is to use
E=XQ_32+YQ_42+Q_52+dQ_25+XQ_35+YQ_45.
The tail-to-tail terms cancel between its two tail sums; bridge-to-tail
terms cancel against dQ_25; tail-to-terminal terms against Q_52; the

part is X(1-X)||g3||^2+Y(1-Y)||g4||^2+
[(C0-d)/2-d^2]||g2||^2+(C0+d)||g5||^2/2.
The identity V_h=V_left+kV_right-2cE+
c[d(2C0+d)-C0^2](||g5||^2-||g2||^2) follows by matching its values and
diagonals. Adding eta(Q_25+Q_52), with
Q_25+Q_52=-d||zeta||^2-(C0+d)<zeta,g5>-<zeta,Kvec>, gives exactly the
displayed M0[g]. Thus the full matrix, rather than only its diagonal,

Put R=X^2/w0+S^2/w1=Rbar/delta,
Rbar=t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)], q=tau/(C0-d), sigma=k-qR,
E0=zeta-Kvec/(C0-d). Square completion gives the exact identity

 M0[g]=w0||U_L||^2+w1||V_L||^2
 +sigma(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)
 +q||S sqrt(w0/w1)U_R-X sqrt(w1/w0)V_R||^2

The cross term in the fourth square is -2qXS<U_R,V_R>, and its other
terms equal q[R(w0||U_R||^2+w1||V_R||^2)-||Kvec||^2], verifying the
identity. For quantitative bounds, the increasing polynomial

Consequently k>339/61>11/2 and
qR<(7/2)C0/[(29/50)^2 C0^2]=8750/(841C0)<3, giving sigma>5/2.
Also w0,w1>3delta, sigma w0,sigma w1>(15/2)delta, and

To fill every missing multiplier, let K0=6I-ee^T and

The chart's starting coordinates are only
N_32=N_35=2cX+epsilon, N_42=2cY+epsilon, all other17 equal epsilon.

## 4. Frozen numerical specification

Implementation: run.wl, executed ONCE by the existing Wolfram evaluator.
All machine mathematics, including model construction, initial arrays,
factorization, optimization, original readbacks, and encoding, occurs in
that single call. No code is evaluated for calibration beforehand.

Variables: five independent h; all20 off-tree ordered coefficients in
lexicographic order; all15 entries of a lower triangular5-by-5 factor C.
The ten dependent Lambda entries use the exact cuts above. No reflection,
support, repeated row, zero remainder, fixed rank, or one-parameter path
is imposed. The relation h=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y) holds ONLY at the initial point.
Equality constraints are the15 lower triangular entries of B-CC^T=0.
All30 multiplier inequalities remain imposed, including tree entries.

Search box:1/4<=each h_i<=6;0<=each original Lambda_ij<=400 (i!=j);
-100<=each lower factor entry<=100, diagonal>=0; additionally every entry
of the FULL6-by-5 factor JC is in[-100,100]. These are search restrictions,
not proved global bounds. The registered objective is maximize1+sum h_i.

Method: one FindMinimum call on its negative, Method->InteriorPoint,
WorkingPrecision->MachinePrecision, AccuracyGoal->8, PrecisionGoal->8,
MaxIterations->600. Initial point is exactly the algebraic specification
above evaluated at machine precision; its factor is obtained from the
actual positive definite leading block. No jitter, alternate start, SDP,
repair, grid, precision rerun or second optimization is permitted.

Initial abort tests: finite real arrays of the correct dimensions; full
flow, Me, direct-array/chart comparison and full factor residual <=1e-9;
all30 coefficients>=epsilon(1-1e-6); full minimum eigenvalue>=-1e-9;
complement minimum>=1/900-1e-9; leading minimum>=1/5400-1e-9; all search
boxes and factor diagonal signs. Failure aborts without optimization.

Numerical candidate tests: actual original coefficient signs >=0;
full flow and Me infinity norms<=1e-7; full original/factor residual<=1e-7;
full minimum eigenvalue>=-1e-8; original/model matrix discrepancy<=1e-8;
factor diagonals>=0 and all search boxes. Stop early once such a point has
A>Gamma5+1e-6. All negative entries and eigenvalues remain visible. This
is only numerical credibility, never exact PSD or a certificate proof.
No exact reconstruction is planned in this job: complete arrays are saved
for a separately selected next stage if the candidate test succeeds.

Counters: EvaluationMonitor increments an exact callback counter and
throws at2000. StepMonitor increments an exact step counter and performs
one full ORIGINAL-model readback at every accepted step. It throws by598
steps, reserving two of the600 total readbacks for initial and final checks.
The solver's internal iterations also have MaxIterations600. The initial
and returned/final point are checked separately; the best checked point
is retained with its already computed readback, avoiding a redundant call.

Time: one30s charge made before evaluation, no refund. Model build and
optimizer phase has a23s kernel guard with a22s callback wall guard; full
readback and compressed export have an overall26.5s kernel guard and the
evaluator receives timeConstraint27. Reserve3s for local decoding and
persistence, enforced by a local SIGALRM. The core deadline is absolute
from call start, not renewed per iteration. Accounted cap is30 regardless
of early exit; actual kernel, transport wall, and local processing times
are recorded separately. Unknown-time timeout charges the full30 and
closes the ledger. The prior870s conservative reservation becomes900s;
neither number is represented as measured runtime.

Output: final full readback FIRST, then best feasible and initial arrays;
h,Lambda,M,C,JC,J and a fixed orthonormal complement basis; all original
flows, Me, factor and model residuals, signs and full/complement/leading
eigenvalues; counters, solver output, version, status and every received
warning. Objective history is explicitly SAMPLED: step1,every10th step,
and the final readback. Exact total and omitted counts are supplied.
There is no per-callback full-vector dump. The complete last callback
vector is retained as a trial point, without an unperformed readback.
Lossless gzip/base64 chunks preserve the complete bounded payload; raw
received response is saved before parsing. No clipping, truncation,
rounding-away of negative values, or reconstructed missing output.

## 5. Retrieval and authority limits

The shared COMPLETE draft841a70f2ad83841d was read in full and its SHA256
matched4e1f0afaa542bb54c0c4e047e72f9a50df6e60011718bd2a1305b8ffa6f4c6b6.
The derivation above independently reproduces the needed formulas from
accepted originals; the shared draft is not promoted to a fact.
The known exact local BnB-PEP paper2203.07305v5 was read at Lemma3 and
Algorithm1 Stage2 for the factor/interior-point method analogy. No external

## Exact symmetric formula: direct algebra, not a feasibility repair

Set P=LT ONCE. For all indices i,j,
 (P^T)_ij=P_ji=sum_k L_jk T_ki
               =sum_k (T^T)_ik (L^T)_kj=(T^T L^T)_ij.
Therefore D+(P+P^T)/2 is identically the specified original matrix M.
Since D is diagonal, its (i,j) and (j,i) entries agree exactly. Retaining
the upper entry and copying it into the lower position implements this
identity: it does not average an arbitrary nonsymmetric matrix, change
a diagonal, project to the PSD cone, or alter any multiplier.

The new source constructs the symbolic model and every numerical readback
from one product P and its literal transpose. It also separately evaluates
M_old=D+(P+T^T L^T)/2 by the former two-product order, retaining BOTH full
arrays. Each readback includes M_old-M, M_old-M_old^T, M-M^T, the difference
between the unmirrored one-product formula and the mirrored representation,
and the full spectrum of M_old. This is a numerical discrepancy diagnostic,
not a claim about unseen arrays from the failed old job. A tiny new
M_old-M discrepancy together with successful factorization would support
floating evaluation asymmetry as the implementation explanation; it could
not recover or prove the actual old job's unobserved matrix.

## Pre-factor evidence and failure behavior

The new readback first validates h and Lambda dimensions and finite-real
entries, then assembles all original matrices. BEFORE Cholesky it saves and
emits a separate JSON-safe compressed stream EQ46_INITIAL containing h,
Lambda,T,L,D,P,M,M_old, the unmirrored one-product matrix, all original flow
and null residuals, symmetry and discrepancy arrays, full/leading/complement
spectra, the old-form spectrum, all30 coefficient signs and original boxes.
A later factorization or final encoding failure cannot erase this emitted
snapshot. If an earlier invalid array or spectral result is encountered,
available values are retained with explicit error tags and no optimization.

Original prechecks must pass before factoring: flow and both null residuals
<=1e-9, assembly discrepancy<=1e-9, zero bitwise symmetry/mirroring residual,
all30 coefficients>=epsilon(1-1e-6), original step/coefficient boxes, minimum
full eigenvalue>=-1e-9, leading>=1/5400-1e-9 and complement>=1/900-1e-9.
These retain the previous criteria and add the independent assembly guards.
Cholesky uses TargetStructure->Dense solely to specify its return container.
Check catches a factorization message as $Failed. Its result must be a real
finite5-by-5 matrix before any transpose, entry extraction, Thread or Diagonal
on that result. Failure stops cleanly with its tagged output. The original
full-factor residual<=1e-9 and direct/chart-array error<=1e-9 remain required
before optimizer initialization. No tolerance is weakened.

Every export passes a recursive JSON conversion that retains strings, lists,
associations, finite ordinary real numbers, booleans and Null. Exact rational
metadata are tagged with their exact InputForm; every other symbolic/failure/
nonfinite expression is explicitly tagged with its InputForm. No unsupported
expression is sent unguarded to RawJSON. If gzip/base64 export fails after JSON
succeeds, a literal plain-JSON fallback is emitted. A footer is printed as
EQ46_FOOTER_JSON=... rather than returned as an escaped JSON string. All raw
received messages and the full encoded response are saved before decoding;
both compressed bytes and decompressed bytes are retained even if the decoded
text is literal $Failed or invalid JSON. Footer processing is independent of
payload success. Final status reflects missing or malformed data honestly.

## Unchanged optimizer and explicit stopping rules

If every initial check passes, run at most ONE FindMinimum on -(1+sum h),
Method->InteriorPoint, WorkingPrecision->MachinePrecision, AccuracyGoal8,
PrecisionGoal8, MaxIterations600. The initial start and constraint-building

expression is algebraically identical, proved above. No dry run or independent

The separately allocated implementation repair passed every original initial
check and reached its ONE authorized optimizer invocation. It did not produce
a sufficient-capacity seed. The best originally checked numerical capacity was
9.694142701216034, below Gamma5=7977607/806000. The original exact-profile goal
remains OPEN. There is no new accepted fact or exact feasibility claim.

solver convergence return: status `invalid_original_step_readback_abort`, solver
return `$Aborted`. The final original two-product evaluation had a tiny complex
conjugate eigenvalue pair. Every complete initial/final original numerical array
and every received message was retained. The ledger is CLOSED with no retry.

upper triangle merely represents that exact symmetry. The full proof and all

# R13 handoff: fixed increasing profile remains OPEN

2026-10-09. **UNVERIFIED analytical draft. Grant 9cb17568ad79648c. Original Goal OPEN.** One authorized invocation and two analytical approaches are complete. No full PSD certificate and no actual negative-D_u trajectory were obtained. Read the complete PROOF_ATTEMPT.md before using either result below.

The original target remains `lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1`, or a certified full-class asymptotic separation, with W=2R and the exact all-integer Zhang--Jiang denominator B_n. Preserve every finite dimension, original selected minimizer/radius, actual endpoint, predetermined nonnegative schedules, and the SAME enclosing parameters throughout each H_m. This packet does not resolve that target.

## Fixed data

For each enclosing m>=1536:

    epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j), k_j=a c^j,
    s_j=sum H_j, S=s_m, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,
    Bcert=1+(32/17)S,
    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i (i<n),
    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.

No weights were retuned. The nonnegative Q convention, actual Bmat, original-radius dominance functional D_u, and FULL remainder R are those in TASK.md.

## Result 1: the positive interval cone is excluded at these new weights

This is a new derivation; the old b_i=h_i result was not reused outside its hypotheses. It covers arbitrary finite positive sums of the explicit variable-step interval modes, including m-dependent coefficients and supports. Those modes have diagonal `H=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T`. It does NOT cover every general C or every possible diagonal-H C.

Let `delta_i=u_i-h_i`, and use the mode quantities q_i, B_i^nu, E_i^nu defined in the proof. The FULL diagonal is

    R_ii=q_i-(h_i-1)^2 q_(i+1)
         -delta_i[2(h_i-1)+delta_i].

The exact generalized prefix identity is

    q_k+sum_(i<k)R_ii
      =(T_k+1)^2-2sum_nu B_k^nu E_k^nu+Xi_k,

where, writing s=T_k and V, W3, K2 for the physical prefix sums of h_i^2, h_i^3, and h_i^2 T_i^2,

    Xi_k=[(s+1)(s^2-V)/2-(s^3-W3)/3]/S-K2/(4S^2)
         <=s^3/(6S)+s^2/(2S).

At the middle bridge, `s=s_(m-1)`, `K=k_(m-1)`, `S=2s+1+K`, and `delta_k=s(1+K)/(2S)`. The positive-mode cut equations and FULL PSD necessity imply

    K^2[1+2(s+1+K)/(s+1-K)]+2K delta_k
      <=(s+1)^2+s^3/(6S)+s^2/(2S).

With r=sqrt(2)-1, the literal enclosing-m limit forces `1+r^3<=1+r/6`, impossible because `6r^2>1` (equivalently `289>288`). Thus this entire cone fails for all sufficiently large inherited m. This already uses the full diagonal after Laplacian cancellation, so retaining signed diagonal terms and their joint control cannot repair a member of that cone.

A free matrix direction/family exclusion is not a realizable-function counterexample. General nonseparable circulations remain open. No actual column-matching C with PSD R was constructed.

## Result 2: broad actual Huber inner phases all pass

For the fixed scalar Huber function with gradient clip[-1,1] and original minimizer 0, the ACTUAL map obeys

    T_(H_j)([-k_j,k_j]) subset [-a beta,a beta]

at every inherited depth j. The proof uses the exact image-radius update, the real physical word, and c<3.

Set J=floor(m/2), `d_j=k_(j+1)-(s_j+1)>0`. Choose any

    Y_J in [max(0,k_J-d_J),k_J],
    Y_(j+1)=s_j+1+k_j-Y_j  for j>=J.

Execute all of H_J from Y_J without prescribing its inner itinerary. Every higher left child and bridge is then actually saturated, lands at -Y_j, and recurses by oddness. All complete H_m trajectories so constructed have `|x_n|=|g_n|<=a beta` and Q_star,i=0.

Uniformly over these inner phases,

    Y_m/S -> r,
    M_m/S^2 -> v=r^2/(1+2sqrt(2)),
    Z/S=r+v/2+q g_n+o(1), q=43/68+v/4,
    Z=sum_i u_i g_i.

The exact original-radius residual is `D_u=2Y_m Z-Z^2-Bcert x_n^2`. With

    eta=r-v/2-q r
       =r[25/68-1/(4(1+2sqrt(2)))]>2r/17,

it satisfies the uniform positive bound

    liminf inf_(admissible inner phases) D_u/S^2
      >=eta(2r-eta)>0.

This includes both parities and an increasing-depth inner phase. It rejects the attempted Huber obstruction, not the fixed dominance profile. Other starts, other functions, and other dimensions are not covered. No stalled-minimizer witness was constructed; parent speculation about one is not used.

## Receipt and recovery boundary

- Invocations 1; analytical approaches 2; mathematical programs/arrays/solvers/formal submissions 0 each; additional agents/models 0.
- Only PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md were written here. No shared administration, Goal mutation, TARGET, verification request, service/worker change, old grant reuse, publication, or finalization.
- New math remains UNVERIFIED. The source hashes were checked in PROOF_ATTEMPT.md. Final output hashes are returned to the parent after readback.
- No per-call token figure is exposed or invented. This grant has no remaining approach credit and must not renew itself.

The required full-class premise `W(H_m)<=1/Bcert` remains missing. A future continuation needs a genuinely different explicit C outside the excluded positive cone, a direct original-space proof, or an actual complete-word negative D_u trajectory. Merely presenting matrix feasibility conditions again would not close that premise. No original-target candidate is ready for formal review.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000566 -->
# R13 fixed increasing weights: two exact scope results, no full certificate

**UNVERIFIED analytical draft. Grant 9cb17568ad79648c. Original target OPEN.**

This packet does not construct an admissible general interpolation matrix with a PSD full remainder, and it does not produce an actual trajectory with negative prescribed dominance residual. The two authorized approaches give the following narrower results.

1. The whole positive cone of variable-step interval modes is excluded for the FIXED increasing physical weights, even when the Laplacian and signed diagonal terms are retained together. This is a new derivation with the actual columns `b_i=u_i`; the old result with `b_i=h_i` is not imported. The contradiction uses a necessary diagonal condition of the FULL remainder, not separately nonnegative diagonal slack after removing the Laplacian.
2. A substantial family of actual symmetric-Huber complete trajectories, allowing arbitrary admissible inner phases at depth `floor(m/2)`, has a uniformly positive residual of order `S^2`. Thus changing the inner phase of that saturated outer recursion cannot provide the requested actual obstruction. This is a calibration on a named family, not a full-class validity result.

The positive interval cone does not exhaust arbitrary admissible cyclic matrices. The first result therefore leaves genuinely nonseparable circulations open. The original minimax question and the fixed increasing dominance functional remain unresolved.

## 1. Sources, authorization, and locked notation

Read completely: OPERATOR.md; PROBLEM.md; R12_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md; R12_EXCESS_WEIGHT_LAPLACIAN_LEAD_20261009.md; R12_PARENT_RANKONE_FLOW_OBSTRUCTION_20261009.md; R12_AB_COUNTEREXAMPLE_HANDOFF_20261009.md; brownian_dominance_certificate_20261009_r12/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md; profile_c_full_certificate_20261009_r12b/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md; and R11_VARIABLE_INTERVAL_CERTIFICATE_LEAD_20261009.md, including its complete Section 5. The mathematical-academic-writing skill and its mathematical-integrity reference were used for evidence and quantifier discipline. The initialized marker is present. No private worker memory was read.

Checked input hashes:

- TASK.md: `abd19f8e86df1dcabcc1afe63cc014ea96ed2277831ee71ed9b4310a743de6e6`.
- R12_EXCESS_WEIGHT_LAPLACIAN_LEAD_20261009.md: `af20644806bbe030993835b5b4258e005a0fc0b30f64835ea68a6c51dd0e6593`.
- R12_PARENT_RANKONE_FLOW_OBSTRUCTION_20261009.md: `870d3f51efa4f7ddb01d6e2632885f578c920e5da7940ba32200d5de77e88eb8`.

The exact original question remains

    lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1,

or a rigorous disproof by a full-class asymptotic separation. Here `W=2R`, and `U_n^ZJ=1/B_n`, where `B_n=2 S_bullet(n)+1` is the EXACT sequence with maximization over all integer splits from PROBLEM.md. The class includes every finite-dimensional convex differentiable L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct initial point, and the actual last iterate of every predetermined nonnegative schedule. Nothing below changes that class, radius, benchmark, or endpoint.

Fix ONE enclosing integer `m>=1536` and retain throughout every child

    c0=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=c0(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_0=empty, H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j).

Write

    k_j=a c^j, s_j=sum H_j, n=2^m-1, h=H_m,
    S=s_m, T_i=sum_(t<i) h_t,
    Bcert=1+(32/17)S,
    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i for i<n,
    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.

`Bcert` is not `B_n`. No physical step is added at query n. Put

    V=sum_(i<n) h_i^2,
    U=sum_(i<n)u_i=5S/4-V/(4S),
    u_n=1+(43/68)S+V/(4S)>0,
    b_i=u_i for i<n, b_n=-U.

Let Bmat have diagonal 1 and entries `(Bmat)_ij=h_j` for `j<i`. For normalized L=1 trajectories use the NONNEGATIVE rows

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||^2,
    Q_star,i=-2(f_i-f_star)+2<g_i,x_i-x_star>-||g_i||^2.

The unchanged functional and full remainder are

    D_u=||x_0-x_star||^2-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2Bcert(f_n-f_star)-||x_0-x_star-sum_i u_i g_i||^2,

    R=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)
      -(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T),

    D_u=sum_(i,j) C_ji Q_ij+g^T R g.                 (1.1)

Here C has zero row sums, nonnegative off-diagonal entries, and column sums b. The Laplacian has weights `w_ij=u_j(u_i-h_i)` for `i<j`. Its diagonal cancels from the full diagonal in (1.1); in particular, if `H=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T`, then

    R_ii=u_i(2-u_i)-H_ii,       i<n.                (1.2)

Equation (1.2) is a necessary PSD test regardless of signs in any decomposition of R.

## 2. Approach 1: exclude the positive interval-mode cone at the actual weights

### 2.1 Explicit index-dependent modes and the precise family

For each atom nu, let `A_i^nu` be nonnegative and nondecreasing, with `A_-1^nu=0`, and let `D_i^nu` be nonnegative and nonincreasing, with `D_(n+1)^nu=0`. Interval-supported atoms are included by extending their cumulative weights by zero or constants outside their support. Absorb a nonnegative combination coefficient into A. Set `r_i=h_i-1` at physical indices. For `i<j`, the actual entries of an atom are

    C_ji^nu=(A_i^nu-A_(i-1)^nu)(D_j^nu+r_j D_(j+1)^nu),
    C_ij^nu=(A_(i-1)^nu+r_i A_i^nu)(D_j^nu-D_(j+1)^nu).

At j=n the dummy r_n multiplies zero. Diagonal entries make row sums zero. All off-diagonal entries are nonnegative. The sum `C=sum_nu C^nu` is an actual, generally index-dependent cyclic coefficient family, although no feasible member with the target columns and PSD remainder is asserted.

Use any finite number of atoms, with arbitrary m-dependent coefficients and supports. This includes positive sums with different interval destinations and does not assume one rank-one destination law.

For this family the symmetric product H is diagonal. Define

    Z_i=sum_nu A_i^nu D_(i+1)^nu,
    q_i=1+2 sum_nu A_(i-1)^nu D_i^nu,
    B_i^nu=sum_(t<i) h_t A_t^nu,
    E_i^nu=sum_(t>=i,t<n) h_t D_(t+1)^nu.

Thus `q_(i+1)=1+2Z_i`, and direct multiplication with the ACTUAL Bmat gives

    H_ii=2[r_i^2 Z_i-sum_nu A_(i-1)^nu D_i^nu].    (2.1)

The off-diagonal cancellation is the interval-mode identity in the source; it follows by writing the upper entry of `C Bmat` as the product of the corresponding forward cumulative factor and backward cumulative factor, and the lower entry as its negative.

### 2.2 The new column equations and full diagonal recurrence

Let `U_i=sum_(t<=i)u_t`, for physical i. The required column sums give

    sum_nu[A_i^nu E_i^nu-D_(i+1)^nu B_i^nu
                       -2r_i A_i^nu D_(i+1)^nu]=U_i. (2.2)

Here is a flow check of (2.2). Across the cut after i, an atom's forward outgoing mass is

    D_(i+1)^nu [B_i^nu+r_i A_i^nu],

and its backward incoming mass is

    A_i^nu [D_(i+1)^nu+E_(i+1)^nu].

Subtract the first from the second and use
`E_i^nu=h_i D_(i+1)^nu+E_(i+1)^nu`. Summing atoms gives the net incoming mass U_i. No individual atom is required to have the target columns.

Put

    delta_i=u_i-h_i=h_i T_i/(2S),
    e_i=U_i-(T_i+h_i)=sum_(t<=i)delta_t.

Combining (1.2) and (2.1) yields the FULL physical diagonal

    R_ii=q_i-r_i^2 q_(i+1)-delta_i(2r_i+delta_i).   (2.3)

The last term is essential. Increasing u changes the physical diagonal as well as the cut equations.

### 2.3 Generalized prefix identity, including the entire correction

For a physical cut k define

    Xi_k=sum_(i<k)[2h_i e_i-delta_i(2r_i+delta_i)].

Then the exact identity is

    q_k+sum_(i<k)R_ii
      =(T_k+1)^2-2sum_nu B_k^nu E_k^nu+Xi_k.       (2.4)

To verify it, use

    B_(i+1)^nu=B_i^nu+h_i A_i^nu,
    E_(i+1)^nu=E_i^nu-h_i D_(i+1)^nu.

Equation (2.2) gives

    sum_nu(B_(i+1)^nu E_(i+1)^nu-B_i^nu E_i^nu)
      =h_i U_i+h_i(h_i-2)Z_i.

Therefore the increment of the right side of (2.4) before adding Xi is

    (1-r_i^2)q_(i+1)-2h_i e_i.

Adding the increment of Xi gives `(1-r_i^2)q_(i+1)-delta_i(2r_i+delta_i)`, which equals the increment of the left side by (2.3). Both sides initially equal 1. This proves (2.4) using only the combined column equations.

For a prefix with total physical mass `s=T_k`, set

    V_<k=sum_(i<k)h_i^2,
    W_<k=sum_(i<k)h_i^3,
    K_<k=sum_(i<k)h_i^2 T_i^2.

The FIXED increasing clock makes the correction explicit:

    Xi_k=[(s+1)(s^2-V_<k)/2-(s^3-W_<k)/3]/S
         -K_<k/(4S^2).                             (2.5)

For completeness, interchanging finite sums gives

    2sum_(i<k)h_i e_i
      =(1/S)sum_(i<k)h_i T_i(s-T_i).

Subtracting `sum delta_i(2r_i+delta_i)` leaves

    (1/S)sum_(i<k)h_i T_i(s+1-T_i-h_i)
      -K_<k/(4S^2).

Now use `sum h_i T_i=(s^2-V_<k)/2` and
`sum h_i T_i(T_i+h_i)=(s^3-W_<k)/3`. These prove (2.5) exactly, without a continuous-clock approximation.

Since `W_<k<=s V_<k` and `K_<k>=0`, (2.5) implies

    Xi_k<=s^3/(6S)+s^2/(2S).                        (2.6)

If the FULL R is PSD, each R_ii is nonnegative. Also `B_k^nu E_k^nu>=0`. Consequently (2.4) gives

    q_k<=(T_k+1)^2+Xi_k.                            (2.7)

No separate nonnegativity of `R-L`, of the signed diagonal in (1.1), or of an invented slack variable has been assumed.

### 2.4 Central cut contradiction

At the central query `k=2^(m-1)-1`, put

    s=s_(m-1), K=k_(m-1), h_k=1+K, S=2s+1+K.

The right physical suffix has total mass s. In each atom, monotonicity of D gives

    E_(k+1)^nu<=s D_(k+1)^nu.

Separating the bridge in (2.2) therefore gives

    U_k<= (s+1-K) Z_k.                              (2.8)

The omitted term `-sum D_(k+1)^nu B_k^nu` is nonpositive. Since `U_k>=s+1+K>0`, existence would force `s+1-K>0`, and

    q_(k+1)>=1+2(s+1+K)/(s+1-K).                   (2.9)

This lower bound deliberately uses only `u_i>=h_i`; retaining the full larger U_k would strengthen it.

At this bridge `delta_k=s(1+K)/(2S)`. Equations (2.3), (2.6), (2.7), and (2.9) imply the following necessary condition for the FULL R to be PSD:

    K^2[1+2(s+1+K)/(s+1-K)]+2K delta_k
      <=(s+1)^2+s^3/(6S)+s^2/(2S).                 (2.10)

The nonnegative `delta_k^2` has only been dropped from the left side.

Let `r=sqrt(2)-1`. The exact inherited-word sum is

    alpha=a/(c-2),
    s_j+1=alpha c^j+(1-alpha)2^j.                   (2.11)

Here alpha tends to 1 and is uniformly positive; `2/c<=2/c0<1`. Along the actual enclosing m, (2.11) gives

    K/s -> r, S/s -> c0=1/r,
    delta_k/s -> r^2/2, s -> infinity.

Divide (2.10) by s^2. Its limiting necessary condition is

    r^2[1+2(1+r)/(1-r)]+r^3 <= 1+r/6.

The identity `r^2+2r=1` makes the bracketed first contribution exactly 1. Thus it would require

    r^3<=r/6.

This is false: `6r^2>1` is equivalent to `17>12sqrt(2)`, and both sides are positive with squares `289>288`. The limiting contradiction has a strict constant gap. All quantities in (2.10) depend only on the literal word and prescribed weights, not on the atoms. Hence every finite positive interval-mode combination fails the full PSD requirement for all sufficiently large enclosing m, even if the number, supports, and weights of the atoms vary arbitrarily with m.

This proves a family obstruction. It does not prove that every admissible C has diagonal H, or that every nonnegative C with diagonal H belongs to this positive cone. Neither assertion is used. General C, and diagonal-H matrices outside this cone, remain unexcluded.

## 3. Approach 2: actual Huber inner-phase variation cannot reject the profile

### 3.1 A complete interval-image bound for the physical word

Use the SAME static function at every query,

    f(x)=x^2/2 if |x|<=1, and |x|-1/2 otherwise,
    g(x)=clip(x,[-1,1]), x_star=0.

It is convex, differentiable, 1-smooth, with unique original minimizer 0, and `Q_star,i=0` at every real query. Every conclusion in this section concerns this function only.

For `h>1`, the image of `[-R,R]` under `x -> x-h clip(x,[-1,1])` is the interval of radius

    Phi_h(R)=(h-1)R,                     0<=R<=1,
    Phi_h(R)=max(R-h,h-1),               R>=1.       (3.1)

This follows from continuity, oddness, and the three affine pieces of the update. In particular it is a statement about actual images, not a freely selected gradient table. For all R>=0,

    Phi_h(R)<=max(R-h,h-1).                         (3.2)

Define `t=a beta`. For every inherited depth j>=1,

    T_(H_j)([-k_j,k_j]) subset [-t,t].              (3.3)

Proof: at j=1, `k_1=beta<1`, so the one step `1+a` gives radius `a beta`. For j>=2, iterate (3.2) through the left H_(j-1). Its output radius is at most

    max(k_j-s_(j-1), k_(j-2)).

To see this, expansion of the scalar max recurrence subtracts the suffix mass from each reset value; each reset value is at most the largest `h-1` in that child, namely k_(j-2).

Both terms are at most `1+2k_(j-1)`. The only nontrivial inequality is

    k_j-s_(j-1)<=1+2k_(j-1),

equivalently `(c-2)k_(j-1)<=s_(j-1)+1`. Formula (2.11) proves it because `0<c-2<1`, so

    s_(j-1)+1>=k_(j-1)/(c-2)>=(c-2)k_(j-1).

Here `c<3` holds throughout the stated parameter range. The bridge of length `1+k_(j-1)` maps every interval of radius at most `1+2k_(j-1)` into `[-k_(j-1),k_(j-1)]`, by (3.1). The induction hypothesis on the final child then proves (3.3). In particular t<1, so every endpoint in (3.3) lies in the actual quadratic region.

### 3.2 Many admissible inner phases and exact outer execution

Set `J=floor(m/2)`. Define

    d_j=k_(j+1)-(s_j+1)
       =(beta-alpha)c^j-(1-alpha)2^j.

For the actual parameters, `0<alpha<1`,

    d_1=epsilon[2/(c0(1+epsilon))-c0 epsilon]>0,
    d_(j+1)=c d_j+(c-2)(1-alpha)2^j>d_j.            (3.4)

Choose ANY base start

    Y_J in [max(0,k_J-d_J), k_J].                   (3.5)

Execute the full physical H_J from that Y_J; no inner itinerary is prescribed. For j>=J define the outer starts by

    Y_(j+1)=s_j+1+k_j-Y_j.                          (3.6)

Let `e_j=k_j-Y_j`. At the base, `0<=e_J<=d_J`, and (3.6) gives

    Y_(j+1)=s_j+1+e_j,
    e_(j+1)=d_j-e_j.

Using (3.4), induction gives `0<=e_j<=d_j` at every later level. Consequently, at level j+1 the entire left H_j and bridge have actual gradient +1; the bridge query is `1+e_j`, and the bridge lands exactly at `-Y_j`. The remaining child is the negative of the complete H_j trajectory by oddness. This constructs every one of the complete H_m trajectories in (3.5)-(3.6), retaining every physical update and the original minimizer.

By (3.3), regardless of its inner phase, the endpoint satisfies

    |x_n|=|g_n|<=t.                                (3.7)

In particular the endpoint is not replaced by an arbitrary gradient of magnitude 1. Such a replacement would be infeasible for this family.

### 3.3 Uniform moments at the actual enclosing depth

Let the reference critical starts be

    X_1=beta,
    X_(j+1)=s_j+1+k_j-X_j.

The source's exact trajectory proof gives `0<X_j<=k_j`; it also follows from (3.4) with `k_1-X_1=0`. Put

    r=sqrt(2)-1,
    v=r^2/(1+2sqrt(2)).

The needed reference limits are

    X_m/S -> r,
    V_m/S^2 -> v,
    M_m^X/S^2 -> v,                                (3.8)

where `V_j=sum_(H_j) h_i^2` and `M_j^X=sum_(H_j)h_i x_(i+1)` along the reference trajectory. These are limits with the SAME enclosing parameters in all children.

For a direct check of their uniformity, write

    d(c)=(c-1)/(c+1),
    P=alpha c d(c), Q=2(1-alpha)/3, R0=beta-P-Q.

The exact finite-depth formulas are

    X_j=P c^(j-1)+Q 2^(j-1)+R0(-1)^(j-1),
    V_j=(2^j-1)+2alpha(c^j-2^j)
        +a^2(c^(2j)-2^j)/(c^2-2).                  (3.9)

The moment satisfies

    M_(j+1)^X+M_j^X
      =s_j X_(j+1)-(s_j^2+V_j)/2-(1+k_j)X_j.      (3.10)

The left-child term is the sum of `h_i(X_(j+1)-T_i-h_i)`, the bridge term is `-(1+k_j)X_j`, and the right child contributes `-M_j^X`. With

    v(c)=(c-2)^2/(c^2-2),
    w(c)=[2d(c)-(1+v(c))/2]/(c^2+1),

(2.11), (3.9), and (3.10) give

    X_j=d(c)alpha c^j+O(2^j),
    V_j=v(c)alpha^2 c^(2j)+O(c^j+2^j),
    M_j^X=w(c)alpha^2 c^(2j)+O((2c)^j).

All constants are uniform for c in the compact interval
`[c0,c0(1+1/6144)]`. For the last estimate, subtract its stated leading term from (3.10); the resulting error plus its predecessor is `O((2c)^j)`, whose finite geometric sum has the same order uniformly. Finally `d(c0)=r`, `v(c0)=v`, and `w(c0)=v`. This proves (3.8) without exchanging a fixed-depth and enclosing-depth limit.

Now compare the starts (3.5)-(3.6) with X. For j>=J,

    Y_j-X_j=(-1)^(j-J)(Y_J-X_J),
    |Y_J-X_J|<=k_J.

The moment recurrence (3.10) also holds for the Y trajectories at every outer level. Every query of its base H_J has absolute value at most k_J: (3.1) cannot increase the radius beyond the larger of its previous value and the maximum `h-1`, and `k_J>1`. Thus both base moments have absolute value at most `s_J k_J`. Subtracting the outer recurrences gives

    |M_m^Y-M_m^X|=O(k_J S),
    |Y_m-X_m|<=k_J,

uniformly for all choices in (3.5). Since `k_J/S=O(c^(J-m)) ->0`, (3.8) therefore strengthens to

    Y_m/S -> r, M_m^Y/S^2 -> v                    (3.11)

UNIFORMLY over the entire family of chosen inner phases. The physical V_m is independent of the phase and retains (3.8).

### 3.4 Strictly positive residual for every such complete phase

Let `Z=sum_(i=0)^n u_i g_i`. Finite summation by parts on the actual recursion gives

    sum_(i<n)u_i g_i
      =Y_m-(3/2)x_n+M_m^Y/(2S).                    (3.12)

For example, `sum T_i h_i g_i=M_m^Y-S x_n`, which proves (3.12) directly. This uses the last physical step in M and introduces no terminal update.

Set

    q=43/68+v/4.

The exact mass identity in Section 1, (3.7), and the uniform limits (3.11) imply

    Z/S=r+v/2+q g_n+o(1), |g_n|<=t -> r,          (3.13)

where the error is uniform over (3.5). The terminal gradient need not converge; its absolute bound is enough. Since q>0,

    limsup |Z/S-r| <= v/2+q r < r.                (3.14)

The strict inequality has an exact margin. Indeed

    eta=r-v/2-q r
       =25r/68-v(r+2)/4
       =r[25/68-1/(4(1+2sqrt(2)))]
       >2r/17>0,                                 (3.15)

using `r(r+2)=1`.

Here every Q_star,i=0 and the endpoint lies in the quadratic region, so the ORIGINAL functional is exactly

    D_u=2Y_m Z-Z^2-Bcert x_n^2.                    (3.16)

Equations (3.7), (3.11), and (3.14) imply the uniform lower bound

    liminf_(m->infinity) inf_(Y_J in (3.5)) D_u/S^2
      >= r^2-(v/2+q r)^2
       =eta(2r-eta)>0.                             (3.17)

The notation `Y_J in (3.5)` means in the closed interval specified there, not an open interval. The term `Bcert x_n^2/S^2` tends uniformly to zero because the terminal point is bounded. Both parities of m, all admissible inner phases, and the original radius Y_m^2 are included.

Thus this attempted actual-function obstruction fails decisively on its stated complete-word family. It neither proves D_u>=0 on other Huber starts nor establishes any inequality for other functions or dimensions.

## 4. Scope and the remaining exact gap

The matrix approach proves more than failure of one rank-one destination law, but less than failure of every admissible cyclic matrix. Its family is the finite positive cone of the explicitly displayed interval modes. All these matrices have diagonal H. The contradiction concerns the full diagonal R_kk and therefore cannot be evaded inside that family by using the Laplacian to compensate a signed diagonal. Matrices outside the family were not excluded.

The actual-function approach constructs all the claimed physical trajectories and proves positive, rather than negative, D_u on them. It supplies no full-function counterexample. No negative coordinate direction in Section 2 is represented as an actual gradient trajectory.

The parent mentioned an optional fixed-prefix trajectory that could stall at another minimizer; no such trajectory was constructed here. A later parent message reports a pending coercivity argument against fixed-prefix versions near exact Silver. Neither speculation is used in a proof in this packet. No claim about growing-depth stalled trajectories is made.

The missing original-target premise remains an actual original-space certificate valid for the full class:

    W(H_m)<=1/Bcert<=17/[16(1+2S)].

If that premise were proved, the supplied accepted benchmark-comparison fact 931b9653ce0f7201 would give the intended strict 799/800 separation. Neither approach here establishes it. There is no complete original-target object for a finished-object adversarial audit or formal submission.

## 5. Resource and stop record

- Fresh native research invocations: 1, this grant only; requested model/effort gpt-6-astra / ultra.
- Substantive analytical approaches: 2: the positive interval-cone full-matrix test at the fixed increasing weights; the actual symmetric-Huber inner-phase attempt.
- Mathematical programs, generated mathematical arrays, solvers, formal submissions: 0 each.
- Additional agents/models, service or worker changes, old grants reused, truth-store or Goal changes: 0 each.
- Writes: PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md in this packet only. Reads, text searches, hashes, and output readback are administrative file operations.
- One attempted R12b source path, `profile_c_cyclic_certificate_20261009_r12b/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`, returned `Cannot find path ... because it does not exist.` A bounded filename search located `profile_c_full_certificate_20261009_r12b/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`, which was then read completely. No success is attributed to the failed read.
- Parent messages were coordination within the existing invocation. No exact per-call token receipt is exposed, and none is invented.

The bounded two-approach grant is exhausted. Stop after the two output readbacks and final hashes. No coefficient replacement, new model, mathematical execution, formal request, or automatic renewal is authorized by this packet.

# max71 finite-row redistribution handoff

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED; original Goal OPEN.
The single allocated invocation is ready to return. Actual outer-loop exit
and completion counters have not been read or changed.

Complete shared proof-attempt: `b29aef4e7da1bade`.
Full-evidence counterexample: `587f6314446f51bf`.

The complete self-contained manuscript is
[joint_compression_and_three_screens.md](/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/finite_row_redistribution_sol_20261002/joint_compression_and_three_screens.md).

`3e4b8b56d412df5e5110bb7b0c35ce65d9e905b75080d14eb1aba39d7eb077bc`.
The administrative receipt and manifest record the saved shared status and
evidence identity; neither is mathematical verification.

The useful new structure is the exact common-gradient compression of the
full original quadratic under arbitrary finite-row redistribution. With child
STEP SUM A, PHYSICAL bridge d, t=A+d, S=2A+d, backward and forward totals
B,F, backward left-destination moment pi_B and forward right-destination
moment pi_F, its diagonals are

    Gamma_L=B+pi_B-tF+sum_L(1+p)xi,
    Gamma_R=1+2A+F+pi_F+sum_R(1+p)xi,

and its cross coefficient is

    J=t*nu_R+(t-1)*B-pi_B-F-pi_F.

At tight Huber contact with xi=0, the necessary moment equation is
pi_B-tF-pi_F=2S*nu_L-2t. All optimum mixed terms and all original
within-child finite rows are included. These formulas are unverified draft
claims and are not a lift theorem.

Three concrete laws are completely specified and screened:

* **Bridge destination:** s=(A+d)/(3A+2d), right optimum scale theta=1-s,
  contextual left primitive array, and backward rows to the bridge. It has
  an explicit full original-Silver PSD calibration at every depth and
  positive actual final coarse curvature. Its exact full lift retains the
  left source remainder. An H4 vector with signs ++++ at0--3 and ----
  at8--11, zero elsewhere, has zero physical displacement and terminal
  gradient but negative source value. Embedded in the top left child with
  x0=0 and right gradients0, it defeats every objective level q.
* **Backward bridge fan:** signed deletion of the entire left inherited
  finite block, replaced by K_(bridge,i)=nu_i=2h_i/(2S-p_i).
  Every final sign and value flow holds, and the one-step base is PSD.
  The isolated original gradient test forces q>=nu_i/4, hence
  zeta>h_i/2 and q>3/250 on the actual large left-root step at m>=1536.
  This law already fails its sharp original-Silver H3 baseline.
* **Retained objective with forward correction:** keep the left finite
  objective block, add K_(i,bridge)=2s*mu_i and adjust its optimum weights.
  Full signs, flow and algebraic contact hold, with the same one-step base.
  Its actual H2 diagonal is C00=1-2b(b+d)/(3b+2d)<0; the defect persists
  down the original left copies. A final-only signed reroute of right
  terminal edges through the optimum makes the right finite block a
  nonscalar copy and pays less than D/100 in exact Huber price. It vanishes
  on the failed original g0 direction, so the full matrix remains negative.

These are certificate-family failures. No direction above is asserted to be
an interpolation-feasible primal witness. There is no new W lower bound,
full-class upper bound or general-dual infeasibility theorem. The accepted
scalar B_n/D<237/250 at m>=1536 retains its original scope and still lacks
a valid full-class upper to pair with it.

The missing construction must pay both zero-displacement/zero-terminal
within-child innovations and isolated large-step gradient directions while
maintaining the coarse moments and all final signs. The attempted forward
contact repair has a negative position cost; the displayed right-terminal
reroute cannot change a left-only defect. A positive coarse assignment or
an unrestricted Schur reformulation is not a completed bridge.

Exactly THREE laws screened. ZERO mathematical programs, numerical
evaluations, arithmetic replays, executed recurrences, solver calls,
benchmark DP, formal calls, fact_submit, new facts, installations, consults
or subagents. One Matlas query and one fallback web call with two queries
were used for proof-method background. The exact existing primary Silver
PDF/text and its proof were reread and copied into this directory's
downloads; no new source theorem was imported.

Administrative errors and corrections: broad initial displays were truncated,
and decisive files were reread in bounded output. One editorial patch failed
with 'Failed to find expected lines'; exact context was reread and the patch
applied. Manual review corrected (t+1)F to tF in the general contact-moment
equation before the shared manuscript was published. The three base laws
have F=0 at their top joins; their screens were unchanged.

Governing task: TASK.md, guidance d2b22c9958cce28e; later guidance
ca0fd834c90cd90a explicitly leaves max71 unchanged. Sol/max policy,
consult OFF. All older computational/formal grants remain closed.
No service, process, resource ledger, finalization, revocation, publication
outside shared stores or shutdown action was taken. No automatic renewal,
additional repair or formal gate is claimed or requested in this handoff.
