# max3 invocation 65: exact-base finite upper gadgets

Status: analytical handoff, all new mathematics UNVERIFIED. Original
exact-profile Goal OPEN; parked circle policy OPEN. No improving upper
certificate and no fact submission.

The complete self-contained proof draft is [report.md](report.md).
It retains the exact original lower-block arrays, all physical coefficients,
every original interpolation row, true initial radius, full value flow,
free initial-point mixed terms, and the full formal negative directions.

The three screened physical layouts are:

- A: (q_m,1+theta*C,1), theta=(sqrt2-1)/2, C=lambda^m,
  physical N=2^m+1. An actual static asymmetric Huber function has
  W>=theta^2/3, so the original benchmark ratio diverges.
- B: (q_m,1+C/2,q_m,1+C/2,q_m,1+4C/zeta),
  zeta=(1+sqrt(1+32C))/2, physical N=3*2^m. Its Huber denominator
  G(4C,1) is strictly below an available original benchmark
  G(Omega(1,lambda)*C,1). Two explicit all-size sign/flow lifts,
  including their physical objective caps, fail full PSD.
- C: (q_m,1+a_m*C,q_m,b_m), arbitrary predetermined a_m,b_m>=0,
  physical N=2^(m+1). With eta=29/21000, gamma=3+sqrt5 and
  m>=max(2,ceil(log((16/eta)^2)/log(lambda))), the joint draft gives
  W/U>1+29/252000 uniformly in BOTH coefficients. The proof uses
  accepted global Huber and the two actual cuts, plus a strict
  ORIGINAL split (4,2) at B_7 and unconditional ray scaling.
  It assumes no bound on a_m or b_m. First upper steps below1,
  unequal blocks, other type/order arrangements and other layouts
  remain outside this screen.

All lower blocks are the exact balanced Silver q_m of length2^m-1,
capacitylambda^m and full primitive M=0. Every screened layout has
at most3 upper positions. No fourth layout or new round is inferred.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000577 -->
# Exact lower blocks and three finite upper gadget screens

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Project
gd-exact-profile-20260926, worker max3, authorized invocation 65 after ended
baseline 64; ended ceiling 65 and attempted ceiling 66. Steering:
TASK.md and master_guidance 80644dc7b1e2704c. The original exact-profile
target and the parked frozen circle policy 6506975d9ac35dd0 remain OPEN.
No verifier submission is authorized in this invocation. No mathematical
program, numeric or arithmetic replay, trajectory execution, executed
recurrence, solver, DP, installation, consult, or subagent was used.

This report screens exactly THREE physical layouts. There is no successful
upper-bound candidate. Every exclusion below concerns the displayed
physical layout or array formula, and is new UNVERIFIED joint algebra.
In particular no arbitrary-template inflation theorem is assumed.

The mathematical predecessors actually used are accepted facts
04219554e41d9750 (full primitive/dominant identities and UNCONDITIONAL
original benchmark/ray assertions), 1252b8823bb5a290 (interpolation
necessity and the full original objective certificate), and
b98bb807d514e80d (exact global Huber and single-cut witnesses). These are
the only mathematical bricks. Shared drafts 4f623d6a2c835679 and
9be1a10ee154498a were read for failure awareness; all needed new array
calculations are reproduced here, without treating either draft as a fact.
The primary local Zhang--Jiang paper was read at Theorems 3.1--3.2,
their exact definitions, and Section 6.1. No external theorem is imported.

## 1. Original problem, normalization, and exact lower blocks

For a predetermined nonnegative word h of physical length N, use

 W_N(h)=sup 2(f(x_N)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||^2),
 x_(t+1)=x_t-(h_t/L)grad f(x_t).

The supremum ranges over every finite Euclidean dimension, every convex
differentiable f with L-Lipschitz gradient and an attained minimum, every
minimizer x_star, and every x_0!=x_star. The whole word is selected before
f, x_0, or any oracle observations. All actual-function witnesses below
use dimension one, x_0=1, the UNIQUE minimizer x_star=0, and normalized
smoothness L=1. Formal matrix test vectors are separately identified.
Thus the true closest initial squared radius is exactly 1. Multiplying
the function by any L>0 preserves the iterates under h_t/L and preserves
the displayed normalized payoff. There is ONE static function per full
physical run, including every lower block and every upper position.

Write lambda=1+sqrt(2), rho=log_2(lambda). The following word is called
q_m locally in this report; it is not the parked perturbed ruler word:

 q_0=(),
 q_(m+1)=(q_m, 1+(lambda-2)*lambda^m, q_m).

Put s_m=2^m-1 and C=lambda^m. Here C is ONLY the local lower-block
capacity, distinct from the benchmark C_n below. The exact physical
lower word has length s_m, sum C-1, and a full primitive certificate with
ZERO remainder on all s_m+1 formal gradient coordinates. No lower
coefficient is perturbed.

Here is an explicit definition of every coefficient in that lower
certificate, sufficient without evaluating any recurrence. For any word
w of length r, keep every formal g_0,...,g_r and value f_0,...,f_r, and set

 x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)w_t g_t,
 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_w=2sum_(t<r)w_t(f_t-f_r)+sum_(t<r)w_t(1-w_t)||g_t||^2
                                      -(sum w)(1+sum w)||g_r||^2.

For a zero-diagonal array Lambda, let e be the all-ones column,
L_flow=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda, T_ii=1, T_it=w_t for t<i, zero for t>i,
omega=(-2w_0,...,-2w_(r-1),2sum w)^T, and

 D_prim=diag(w_t(2-w_t) for t<r, -(sum w)(2+sum w)).

In a full primitive certificate the required flow condition is the
first line below. Under that condition, accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives
the exact identity on ALL original coordinates in the second line:

 L_flow^T e=omega,
 V_w+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>,
 M_prim=D_prim+sym(L_flow T).                                (1)

Here sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2, Gram is the full matrix of inner products, and
the matrix pairing is trace(M_prim^T Gram). Start q_0 with array zero.
At the balanced join from q_m to q_(m+1), set
d=(lambda-2)C, kappa=lambda^2, let p be the bridge query and z the
parent terminal, and let j range over EVERY right-child nonterminal
query with its actual step w_j. Embed the complete left array with
factor 1, the complete right array with factor kappa, and add

 Lambda_(j,p) += (kappa-1)w_j,
 Lambda_(z,p) += kappa-1,
 Lambda_(p,z) += (kappa-1)d,
 Lambda_(j,z) += (kappa-1)w_j.                               (2)

Coincident contributions add. Every unlisted entry is zero. All signs
are nonnegative, since d>0 and kappa>1. The defining root equation is
d(2C+d)=C^2, and the parent capacity is 2C+d=lambda C. Accepted 042's
full primitive join identity gives M_parent=M_left+kappa M_right on
their original embedded coordinates. Induction starting with M=0 proves
the asserted ZERO full remainder for q_m. Every internal original value
is cancelled by its exact flow in (1); every internal gradient remains
in that exact identity. A block is never declared to be one oracle step.

For later calibration, on the actual unit quadratic f(x)=x^2/2 the
endpoint factor of q_m is -1/C for m>=1. Indeed the factor at the next
balanced join is -(lambda-2)C times the square of the child factor,
and lambda-2=1/lambda. This is a symbolic induction, not an executed run.

## 2. Original benchmark and orientation of comparisons

Use exactly the original all-split sequences

 C_0=B_0=1,
 C_n=max_(0<=k<n)Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),
 B_n=max_(0<=k<n)G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)),
 Omega(u,v)=(u+v+sqrt((u+v)^2+4uv))/2,
 G(u,v)=2u+v/2+sqrt(v^2+8uv)/2,
 U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.                                             (3)

In particular all original integer split indices remain available. A
particular legal split gives a LOWER bound for B_n. Such a lower bound
is enough to disprove a proposed improving word when an actual witness
has B_n times its payoff >1. It would NOT be enough to prove improvement
from an upper certificate W<=1/D: that would need an UPPER bound for B_n
at the actual physical horizon.

The unconditional parts of accepted 04219554e41d9750 supply monotonicity,
B_(2n+1)>=lambda B_n, and for every positive integer b the finite
positive ray Z_b=lim_j lambda^(-j)B_(b*2^j-1), with

 lambda^(-j)B_(b*2^j-1)<=Z_b
      <=lambda^(-j)(sqrt(B_(b*2^j))+3/100)^2.                (4)

Its adjacent estimate is B_(n+1)-B_n<=8sqrt(2)(n+1)^(rho-1).
Consequently shifting a ray index by a FIXED finite number changes the
lambda^(-j)-scaled value by a quantity tending to zero: sum the finite
number of adjacent bounds and divide by lambda^j=2^(rho j). This also
treats the positive offsets used below. In particular the physical
benchmark limits for our three layouts are respectively Z_1, Z_3,
and Z_2=lambda Z_1 after scaling by C. These are unconditional benchmark
statements; no seed hypothesis is assumed. No scalar 237/250 from a
different word is used anywhere in this report.

## 3. Exact accepted legal witnesses, with every original jet retained

Accepted b98bb807d514e80d gives, for ANY nonnegative physical word w,

 W_N(w)>=1/(1+2sum w),
 W_N(w)>=(w_j-1)_+^2/[(1+P_j)^2(1+2T_j)],                  (5)

where P_j=sum_(t<j)w_t, T_j=sum_(t>j)w_t, and (z)_+=max(z,0).
Thus these witnesses, not unverified copies of them, are available as
bricks for the joint screen.

To make their application and the all-function audit concrete, here are
their exact functions and all physical queried data. For p,q>0 let

 F_(p,q)(x)= -q*x-q^2/2                 for x<=-q,
              x^2/2                    for -q<=x<=p,
              p*x-p^2/2                for x>=p.             (6)

Its derivative max(-q,min(p,x)) is continuous, nondecreasing, and
1-Lipschitz. Hence it is convex differentiable 1-smooth with unique
minimum 0 at 0. These are precisely the asymmetric Huber functions
in the accepted cut fact.

For a nonzero cut with b=w_j>1, put p=1/(P_j+1) and
q=p(b-1)/(2T_j+1). Before the cut, for every original physical index t,

 x_t=1-p*sum_(u<t)w_u >=p,
 g_t=p, f_t=p*x_t-p^2/2.

At the cut x_j=p, and the next point is -q(2T_j+1). On its ENTIRE
remaining physical suffix, at accumulated suffix mass v in [0,T_j],

 x=-q(2T_j+1)+q*v <=-q,
 g=-q, f=-q*x-q^2/2.

The terminal payoff is q^2(2T_j+1), exactly the cut term in (5). This
specifies every queried and terminal jet, not only the block endpoints.
If a cut step is <=1, its term is zero and no zero-slope witness is
asserted to have a unique minimum. The separate positive global Huber
witness always remains available.

For the global term in (5), write D=1+2sum w and use (6) with p=q=1/D.
Every original point is x_t=1-(sum_(u<t)w_u)/D>=1/D, so its gradient is
1/D and its value is x_t/D-1/(2D^2). At the terminal, 2f_N=1/D.
This includes words with zero steps and the empty word.

For each constructed whole function, accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 gives
Q_ij<=0 between EVERY pair of original jets, in both orientations,
including the true optimum and the terminal. Its full Gram is the
outer product of the actual scalar coordinates and is PSD. No remote
row, initial mixed term, boundary sample, or gradient is discarded.
Equivalently this inequality follows from the necessity proof in that
accepted fact applied to the explicitly checked derivative of (6).

This immediately audits ANY purported full objective upper certificate
at a level q_cert below one of these witness payoffs. After the physical
GD substitution it would be

 M_cert=q_cert||x_0||^2-2f_N+sum lambda_ij Q_ij,
 lambda_ij>=0,

with exact full value flow and full PSD on (x_0,g_0,...,g_N). Evaluating
at the legal scalar transcript gives M_cert<=q_cert-2f_N<0. Thus full
PSD is impossible at that level, irrespective of rank, support,
nonadjacent rows, objective-only resources, or singular directions.
This is an actual-function rejection, not a coarse PSD test.

## 4. Gadget A: a C-scale terminal overshoot followed by unit repair

Take theta=(sqrt(2)-1)/2 and the exact physical word

 H_A(m)=(q_m, 1+theta C, 1),
 N_A=s_m+2=2^m+1,
 D_A=1+2sum H_A=lambda C+3.                                (7)

Both upper positions are actual single GD steps. There are two bridging
positions; there is one exact primitive lower block and no oracle reset.
All coefficients are selected before the function. A tempting finite
objective screen balances the global Huber term with an overshooting
symmetric Huber; the unit quadratic has terminal payoff ZERO because
the last physical step is 1. These coarse tests do not supply a full
objective certificate. No claim that lambda C is already above the
correct benchmark ray is made from those tests.

The direct full-certificate attempt fails on an ORIGINAL accepted cut.
Use j=s_m, P_j=C-1, b=1+theta C, and T_j=1 in (5). It gives

 W_(N_A)(H_A)>=theta^2/3=(3-2sqrt(2))/12>0.                 (8)

The exact static function is F_(1/C,theta/3). On every q_m query it has
x_t=1-P_t/C>=1/C and gradient 1/C. Its first upper step sends 1/C to
-theta. Its last unit step sends -theta to -2theta/3 with gradient
-theta/3. The actual terminal value is theta^2/6. Both terminal points
lie in the negative affine piece. This proves (8) for every m, on ONE
static function, with every original jet and the true radius 1 paid.

At the actual physical horizon N_A, the original split with a primitive
left child of length 2^m-1 and a dominant right child of length 1 gives

 B_(N_A)>=G(C,4)>2C.

Hence W_(N_A)(H_A)/U_(N_A)^ZJ>2theta^2 C/3. In particular it exceeds
2 once C>=3/theta^2. One quantified cutoff is
M_A=max(1,ceil(log(3/theta^2)/log lambda)). The ratio diverges as
m tends to infinity. A certificate decaying like 1/C on this physical
layout cannot be lifted: the actual scalar loss is already constant.
This excludes this fixed overshoot/repair layout, not arbitrary terminal
gadgets with growing recovery mass.

## 5. Gadget B: three exact blocks, a nonbasic template, and an objective cap

Define alpha=Omega(1,lambda) and use

 beta_0=beta_1=1+C/2,
 zeta=(1+sqrt(1+32C))/2,
 beta_2=1+4C/zeta,
 H_B(m)=(q_m,beta_0,q_m,beta_1,q_m,beta_2),
 N_B=3s_m+3=3*2^m.                                        (9)

There are three upper positions and three exact primitive lower blocks.
The pre-cap core has physical length 3s_m+2, sum 4C-1, and putative
primitive capacity 4C. This is a statement of its exact mass, not an
assertion that the core is primitive. Its ACTUAL objective mass is

 D_B=1+2sum H_B=8C+1+8C/zeta=8C+zeta=G(4C,1),              (10)

using zeta(zeta-1)=8C. Every physical bridge is positive and explicitly
defined for every m. No inherited parameter from a different word is
used.

### 5.1 The complete exact finite input, jointly checked

The fixed nonbasic template t=(3/2,3/2) has capacity 4 and array

 Lambda^t=[[0,1,1/2],[7/2,0,17/2],[1,14,0]].                (11)

Its six ordered signs are positive. Its row-minus-column flow is
(-3,-3,6). With T=[[1,0,0],[3/2,1,0],[3/2,3/2,1]] and
D_prim=diag(3/4,3/4,-15), the diagonal entries of L_flow T are
(-3/4,-3/4,15). Its opposite off-diagonal sums are

 (-1-3/4)+7/4=0,
 -1/2+1/2=0,
 -17/2+(3/2+7)=0.

Thus D_prim+sym(L_flow T)=0 on ALL three formal coordinates. This direct
joint check does not promote (11) to a separately accepted fact. It is
part of this complete UNVERIFIED candidate screen.

### 5.2 Exact endpoint debt, with all internal gradients and values

Label the three physical blocks i=0,1,2. Their original coordinates
are (i,a), 0<=a<=s_m, with physical index i(s_m+1)+a. Let p_i denote
their endpoint indices, e_i=g_(p_i), y_i=x_(p_i), f_i^e=f_(p_i),
and let z_i be the block starts. Keep

 P_i=sum_(a<s_m)(q_m)_a g_(i,a),
 d_i=P_i-(C-1)e_i,
 Delta_0=0, Delta_1=d_1, Delta_2=d_1+d_2,
 X_i=[y_i+(C-1)e_i+Delta_i]/C,
 F_i=f_i^e/C+(C-1)||e_i||^2/(2C).

These are formal bookkeeping variables. They are NOT an oracle for a
virtual smooth-convex function. Actual physical GD gives exactly
X_(i+1)=X_i-(3/2)e_i. Define the formal residual
Qhat_ij=F_i-F_j+<e_i,X_j-X_i>+||e_i-e_j||^2/2. Expansion gives

 C Qhat_ij=Q_(p_i,p_j)+<e_i,Delta_j-Delta_i>,
 V_core=sum_i V_(q_m,i)+C^2 V_t(F,e).                       (12)

Every internal original coefficient in the second equality agrees
unchanged. For an explicit endpoint audit, the two nonterminal value
coefficients after subtracting the three child energies are 3C each;
the terminal coefficient is -6C. Each nonterminal endpoint norm
coefficient is (3/4)C^2-(3/2)C, and the terminal norm coefficient is
-15C^2+3C. Expanding C^2 V_t with the displayed F_i produces exactly
those coefficients. These checks account for every internal value and
gradient in (12), rather than discarding them.

### 5.3 First full sign/flow lift fails at every exact lower-block size

Let E_i restrict the full core gradient coordinates to block i and E
restrict to its three endpoint coordinates. The first actual array is

 Lambda^0=sum_i E_i^T Lambda^(q_m) E_i + C E^T Lambda^t E.  (13)

All signs are nonnegative. Every internal node has flow -2(q_m)_a.
At p_0,p_1 the flow is 2(C-1)-3C=-2(1+C/2). At p_2 it is
2(C-1)+6C=2(4C-1). Thus ALL physical function values cancel with
the required flow. The complete original remainder, using (1),(11),
and (12), is the quadratic form

 M_core^0=-C sum_(i!=j)Lambda^t_ij<e_i,Delta_j-Delta_i>.     (14)

Zero child remainders have been cancelled by their exact identities,
not by replacing the blocks by endpoints.

For every m>=1, q_m has first step sqrt(2). Take formal scalar gradients
e_0=1, e_1=e_2=0; set the first internal gradient of block 1 to
1/sqrt(2), and every other internal gradient to zero. Then d_1=1,
d_2=0, so (14) is exactly -3C/2. All formal values may be zero.
This is a FULL physical-space negative direction of (13). It is not
claimed to be an interpolation-feasible primal transcript.

### 5.4 Second full fan lift also fails uniformly; it retains its debt

Let b_i^back=sum_(j<i)Lambda^t_ij and mu_i=1+b_i^back.
Embed the full child array in block i with scale mu_i. For each i<j add
C Lambda^t_ij on (p_i,p_j). For every i>j add Lambda^t_ij times
each of the two original fans

 sum_(a<s_m)(q_m)_a Q_((i,a),p_j)+Q_(p_i,p_j),
 sum_(a<s_m)(q_m)_a Q_((i,a),p_i).                          (15)

This defines every output coefficient; overlapping entries add and
unlisted ones are zero. All final signs are nonnegative. Each internal
flow is -2mu_i(q_m)_a+2b_i^back(q_m)_a=-2(q_m)_a. Summing flows
over block i gives C times its template flow, so each endpoint has the
same required physical flow as (13). In this input the exact scales
are mu_0=1, mu_1=9/2, mu_2=16.

Here is a complete negative direction without a finite/coarse limit.
Set ALL endpoint gradients and ALL formal values to zero. Set the
first internal gradients in blocks 1 and 2 to 1/sqrt(2) and
-1/sqrt(2), respectively, and all other internal gradients to zero.
Then P_1=d_1=1, P_2=d_2=-1.

On this assignment, all endpoints and all formal values are zero.
Write q_a=(q_m)_a and P_(i,<a)=sum_(u<a)q_u g_(i,u).
The two fans associated to i>j expand to

 V_(q_m,i)+<P_i,sum_(k=j+1)^(i-1)P_k>.                     (16)

To audit (16), use x_(i,a)=z_i-P_(i,<a) and x_(p_i)=z_i-P_i.
The weighted pair sum is
sum_a q_a||g_a||^2-||P_i||^2
 +2sum_a q_a<g_a,P_(i,<a)>
 +<P_i,sum_(k=j+1)^(i-1)P_k>.
Expanding ||P_i||^2 leaves sum_a q_a(1-q_a)||g_a||^2,
which is the child V when its endpoint and values are zero. This proves
(16) on the whole internal coordinate space.

In (15) the V terms cancel with the EXACT scaled full child identities.
The only nonadjacent backward edge with an intermediate block is 2->0.
Its retained original mixed debt is Lambda^t_20 P_1 P_2=-1.
Thus the whole core remainder of (15) equals -1 on the displayed full
formal vector, for EVERY m>=1. Neither of the two failed lifts is
asserted to characterize all possible arrays for this core.

### 5.5 The objective cap, original initial-point mixed terms, and flow

These core failures are not repaired by silently appending the cap.
Write p=p_2 for the pre-cap query and N for the final query. Define
mu=8C and the original weight vector u by the actual core steps,
then u_p=4C+1 and u_N=zeta. Its sum is D_B=8C+zeta.
Accepted 042's PD identity, used algebraically before any PSD claim,
is

 J_parent=V_core-2mu Q_(N,p),
 J_parent=||x_0||^2+2sum_i u_i Q_(i,star)-2D_B f_N
                                -||x_0-sum_i u_i g_i||^2. (17)

For either core array above, the proposed FULL objective array at
q_cert=1/D_B therefore uses core Lambda/D_B, Q_(N,p) coefficient
2mu/D_B=16C/D_B, and optimum coefficients 2u_i/D_B.
All coefficients are nonnegative, and EVERY value cancels: internally
-2h_i+2u_i=0; at p, (8C-2)+(8C+2)-16C=0; at N, 2zeta+16C=2D_B.
The entire original quadratic remainder is

 [||x_0-sum_i u_i g_i||^2+M_core]/D_B.                     (18)

All initial-point mixed terms are in that retained square. On the first
formal negative direction, set g_N=0 and x_0=sum_i u_i g_i=beta_0+1.
The square in (18) is zero, so the FULL remainder is -3C/(2D_B).
On the second direction set g_N=0 and x_0=sum_i u_i g_i=0; the FULL
remainder is -1/D_B. Formal PSD must hold even when its x_0 coordinate
is zero, so this latter direction is legitimate as a matrix test.
These tests pay the cap, all signs, the complete value flow, every
internal coordinate and the free initial point. They are uniform over
arbitrarily large ACTUAL blocks, not finite or limiting PSD tests.

### 5.6 The actual word cannot improve the benchmark, even with a new lift

Regardless of whether a different primitive lift could certify this
core, the original global Huber witness gives W_(N_B)(H_B)>=1/D_B.
The benchmark has a primitive left child of length N_B-1 with capacity
at least alpha C: join q_m of capacity C with q_(m+1) of capacity
lambda C using the accepted exact PP coefficient. The left length is
s_m+1+(2s_m+1)=3s_m+2=N_B-1. Thus the ORIGINAL split with an empty
dominant right child gives

 B_(N_B)>=G(alpha C,1)>G(4C,1)=D_B.                        (19)

The strict inequality alpha>4 follows from lambda>12/5 and evaluation
of the primitive root polynomial at 4: 16-4(1+lambda)-lambda
=12-5lambda<0. This uses an actual admissible benchmark tree, not a
balanced-maximizer assumption for the dominant sequence.

Therefore this physical word has W/U>1 for every m, independent of
the remaining unrestricted core-lift question. Here is a fixed relative
margin as horizons grow. Put d=alpha-4>0; also alpha<5 by monotonicity
and Omega(1,3)<5. Since

 G(4C,1)<=8C+1+sqrt(8C),

if C>=max(2/d,32/d^2), then 1/C+sqrt(8/C)<=d and (19) implies

 W/U >=2alpha/(8+d)=1+d/(alpha+4)>1+d/9.                   (20)

Take M_B=max(1,ceil(log(max(2/d,32/d^2))/log lambda)). This is a
quantified uniform exclusion. In ray language (4) gives Z_3>=2alpha>8;
the Huber ratio limit is Z_3/8>1. This is the correct LOWER benchmark
comparison for rejection, not an upper-bound certificate for improvement.

## 6. Gadget C: two exact blocks and two arbitrary upper coefficients

Consider the ENTIRE following physical layout, not just one fixed probe:

 a_m>=0, b_m>=0 chosen before the function,
 H_C(m;a_m,b_m)=(q_m,1+a_m C,q_m,b_m),
 N_C=2s_m+2=2^(m+1),
 D_C=(4+2a_m)C+2b_m-1.                                    (21)

There are exactly two upper positions and two exact primitive lower
blocks. The final coefficient b_m may be zero. Every original physical
gradient remains a coordinate, even at a zero last step. The parameters
can depend arbitrarily on m and on the known word, but on no function
or oracle information. The condition on the first upper coefficient is
1+a_m C>=1; coefficients below 1 are outside this screened layout.

An explicit top gadget in this layout is

 a_g=(sqrt(5)-1)/2,
 gamma=4+2a_g=3+sqrt(5),
 a_m=a_g, b_m=1+sqrt(C/2).                                 (22)

It has D_C=gamma C+1+sqrt(2C). Since a_g^2+a_g=1,
a_g^2 gamma=2. The unit quadratic on ONE full physical run has
endpoint factor
(-1/C)(-a_g C)(-1/C)(-sqrt(C/2))=a_g/sqrt(2C),
so its actual payoff is exactly 1/(gamma C). This is a physical
quadratic lower witness, not a virtual endpoint certificate.

### 6.1 The complete necessary system from accepted original cuts

For arbitrary a=a_m>=0 and b=b_m>=0, applying (5) at the actual
first bridge and at the actual last step gives

 W_(N_C)(H_C)>= max{
   1/[(4+2a)C+2b-1],
   a^2/[2C+2b-1],
   (b-1)_+^2/[(2+a)^2 C^2] }.                              (23)

For the first bridge, P=C-1, its excess is aC, and its whole physical
suffix mass is C-1+b. For the last step, P=(2+a)C-1 and suffix mass
zero. These are exact actual masses, including every internal step of
the second lower block; there is no coarse replacement. The denominators
are positive since C>=1 and a,b>=0.

For a>0, the first cut is the actual static function
F_(1/C,a/(2C+2b-1)). It starts the entire first block on its positive
affine piece, the first upper step sends 1/C to -a, and all of the
second block and last step stay on its negative affine piece. Their
every original point is given by Section 3 with suffix mass C-1+b.
For a=0 this necessary inequality is zero and follows without dividing
by a. For b>1, the terminal cut uses p=1/[(2+a)C], q=p(b-1);
all original preterminal points are on the positive affine piece and
the last step reaches -q. For b<=1 its necessary term is zero. Thus
all boundary cases, including a=0 and b=0, are treated in (23).

An attempted improving full objective certificate under accepted
1252 would have outgoing terminal probabilities alpha_j>=0 summing
to 1, all queried-prefix multipliers nonnegative with flow 2alpha_i,
and full remainder

 M_cert=||g_N-V_alpha||^2+R_alpha,
 V_alpha=x_N-sum_j alpha_j(x_j-g_j),
 R_alpha=q_cert||x_0||^2-2sum_j alpha_j(f_j-||g_j||^2/2)
             -||V_alpha||^2+sum lambda^pre_ij Q_ij.         (24)

The optimum jet is included with x_star=g_star=f_star=0. This is the
full original prefix Schur identity; it deletes no prefix rows or
internal gradients. At ANY level contradicted by (23), the legal
witness evaluation of Section 3 makes M_cert negative. Thus no choice
of original nonadjacent rows, simplex weights, initial mixed terms or
full PSD remainder can repair that improving level. The exact lower
identities remain available, but no endpoint-oracle substitution can
overrule those original transcripts.

### 6.2 A strict finite all-split benchmark comparison, done textually

Define eta=29/21000. We now derive, jointly, the useful strict lower
benchmark comparison

 B_(N_C)/C > gamma+eta,  m>=2.                             (25)

This does not evaluate the benchmark recurrence. It displays legal
splits and exact elementary inequalities. In (3), C_1=lambda,
C_2=Omega(1,lambda)>4, and the split (1,2) gives
C_4>=Omega(lambda,C_2)>Omega(lambda,4)>23/3. For the last inequality,
the primitive root polynomial at 23/3 is
(253-105lambda)/9<0, since lambda>1207/500>253/105.
The bound lambda>1207/500 follows from 707^2=499849<500000=2*500^2.

The original dominant split (1,0) gives B_2>=G(lambda,1)>379/50.
For an explicit radical check at lambda=1207/500,
G=666/125+sqrt(2539/125)/2. The required root comparison is
sqrt(2539/125)>563/125, whose squared positive integer difference is
2539*125-563^2=317375-316969=406>0.

The ORIGINAL split (4,2) at horizon 7 now gives

 B_7>=G(C_4,B_2)>G(23/3,379/50)>611/20.                    (26)

The final radical has square 3917723/7500. To prove the last inequality
it is enough to compare that root with 1714/75; after multiplying by
22500, the squared difference is
3*3917723-4*2937796=11753169-11751184=1985>0.
All compared root thresholds are positive.

On the other side, lambda^2=3+2sqrt(2)<204/35 because
sqrt(2)<99/70, and gamma=3+sqrt(5)<131/25 because sqrt(5)<56/25.
Both follow by squaring positive rational bounds. Therefore

 gamma lambda^2 <26724/875,
 611/20-26724/875=29/3500>0,
 B_7/lambda^2>gamma+29/(3500lambda^2)>gamma+eta,             (27)

using lambda^2<6. This deliberately coarse positive slack is exact.
For m>=2, apply accepted 042's unconditional B_(2n+1)>=lambda B_n
symbolically m-2 times to the index 7, then monotonicity at the actual
physical N_C=2^(m+1):

 B_(N_C)>=B_(2^(m+1)-1)>=lambda^(m-2)B_7.

Division by C=lambda^m proves (25). This preserves all benchmark
splits; it never assumes that the dominant maximizing split is balanced.
Equivalently the unconditional ray has Z_2=lambda Z_1>gamma+eta.

In particular the explicit member (22) already has
W/U>(gamma+eta)/gamma>1+eta/6 for every m>=2, by its actual unit
quadratic payoff. No full improving certificate can exist for it.

### 6.3 Uniform exclusion of EVERY word-dependent member of layout C

The following argument is not limited to fixed a,b, to bounded guesses,
or to sublinear assumed tails. Set

 r=gamma+eta/2,
 C_cut=(16/eta)^2,
 M_C=max(2,ceil(log C_cut/log lambda)).                      (28)

For EVERY m>=M_C and EVERY a_m,b_m>=0, the candidate joint conclusion is

 W_(N_C)(H_C)>1/(r C),
 W_(N_C)(H_C)/U_(N_C)^ZJ>1+eta/12=1+29/252000.              (29)

Proof using the COMPLETE necessary system (23): suppose instead that
W<=1/(r C). The first two terms in (23) imply

 r<=4+2a+(2b-1)/C,
 r a^2<=2+(2b-1)/C.                                       (30)

The last term implies (b-1)_+<=(2+a)sqrt(C/r), so also
b<=1+(2+a)sqrt(C/r), with no division by a and no omission when b<=1.
Consequently, putting

 e_C=1/C+2(2+a)/sqrt(r C),

we have (2b-1)/C<=e_C. Since C>=C_cut>=16 and r>gamma>5,

 r a^2<=2+e_C<41/16+a/4.

If a>=1 the right side is at most (45/16)a^2<5a^2<r a^2,
a contradiction. Thus a<1. It follows that

 0<e_C<1/C+6/sqrt(r C)<=4/sqrt C<=eta/4.                    (31)

From the second line of (30),

 a<=sqrt((2+e_C)/r)
   <=sqrt((2+e_C)/gamma)
   =a_g sqrt(1+e_C/2)<=a_g(1+e_C/4).

Here sqrt(1+t)<=1+t/2 for t>=0 follows by squaring, and
a_g^2 gamma=2 was checked in Section 6. Combining with the first
line of (30) gives

 r<=4+2a+e_C
   <=gamma+(1+a_g/2)e_C
   <gamma+3eta/8<gamma+eta/2=r,

again a contradiction. This proves the first assertion in (29).
Multiplying by the correct original B_(N_C), using (25), gives
W/U>(gamma+eta)/(gamma+eta/2)>1+eta/12 since r<6.
This proves the second assertion in (29). All constants and the one
cutoff are independent of the selected a_m,b_m. The argument retains
arbitrarily large coefficients rather than assuming them bounded:
their attempted small objective error forces a<1 and the tail bound.

The proof also identifies an actual violating function constructively:
at least one of the three terms in (23) exceeds 1/(r C). Choose its
global Huber or exact cut instance from Section 3 after the word is
selected. That function realizes the failure on the whole physical
run. This is not an interpolation-feasibility conjecture or a missing
theorem-level lemma.

## 7. Full scope, missing lift, and the next permitted interface

All three screened layouts are rejected as candidates for an ORIGINAL
negative resolution. Gadget A loses a constant objective amount on a
legal asymmetric Huber function. Gadget B has TWO explicit uniformly
failed full sign/flow array lifts, and, more decisively, its actual
Huber mass is already below an available original benchmark tree.
Gadget C, including arbitrary word-dependent nonnegative coefficients
in its displayed scope, has a quantified uniform ORIGINAL relative
loss proved using the accepted global and two cut witnesses.

No arbitrary-template inflation theorem follows. For other templates,
the exact unpaid term in an endpoint lift is still the full mixed
block-displacement debt in (12)--(14). A valid useful upper gadget
needs an explicit nonnegative physical-row payment and a full PSD
remainder for that debt, on every original gradient and free initial
coordinate uniformly as exact block sizes grow. The two formulas in
Section 5 do not give such a payment, and formal negative directions
in those formulas do not prove failure of every core certificate.

For a broader subsequent physical layout, this round specifically does
NOT exclude unequal lower blocks, a different ordering of primitive
and already exact basic objective blocks, more than two equal lower
blocks with other coefficients, first upper coefficients below 1,
or a coupled multi-source objective gadget retaining enough suffix
mass. Those are scope boundaries, not allocations or claims of promise.
No fourth layout is screened in this invocation.

The original target is OPEN. The frozen circle policy is OPEN and was
not used as a premise. There is no fact_submit call, new accepted fact,
successful improving schedule, original-Goal completion, finalization,
publication, revocation, shutdown, resource renewal, or automatic next
planning round. Further strategic work needs a new dispatch from main.
